@Composable
fun ContactUsScreen(
  contactConfig: ContactConfigEntity,
  modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
  val whatsappDigits = contactConfig.whatsappNumber.filter { it.isDigit() }

  LazyColumn(
    modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 20.dp),
    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
  ) {
    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        SectionHeader(
          title = "Contact Us",
          subtitle = "Official foundation contact details maintained by the administrator.",
        )
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        Card(
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
          colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
          border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
        ) {
          Column(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
          ) {
            ContactRow(
              icon = Icons.Outlined.Person,
              label = "Contact Person",
              value = contactConfig.contactPerson,
            )
            HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant)
            ContactRow(
              icon = Icons.Outlined.Phone,
              label = "Mobile Number",
              value = contactConfig.mobileNumber,
            )
            HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant)
            ContactRow(
              icon = Icons.Outlined.Email,
              label = "Email Address",
              value = contactConfig.emailAddress,
            )
            HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant)
            ContactRow(
              icon = Icons.Outlined.LocationOn,
              label = "Address",
              value = contactConfig.address,
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))

            com.example.ui.components.ContactUsActionButtons(
              mobileNumber = contactConfig.mobileNumber,
              whatsappNumber = contactConfig.whatsappNumber,
              emailAddress = contactConfig.emailAddress,
              tagPrefix = "contact_page",
            )

            if (whatsappDigits.length < 10) {
              Surface(
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant,
                shape = MaterialTheme.shapes.small,
              ) {
                Text(
                  text = "WhatsApp contact button will appear once a WhatsApp number is configured by the administrator.",
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                )
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

@Composable
private fun ContactRow(
  icon: ImageVector,
  label: String,
  value: String,
) {
  Row(
    verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top,
    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
  ) {
    Icon(
      imageVector = icon,
      contentDescription = null,
      tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
      modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
    )
    Column {
      Text(
        text = label,
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
      )
      Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
      Text(
        text = value,
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
      )
    }
  }
}

@Composable
fun LegalListScreen(
  policies: List<LegalPolicyEntity>,
  onOpenPolicy: (String) -> Unit,
  modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
  LazyColumn(
    modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 20.dp),
    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
  ) {
    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        SectionHeader(
          title = "Legal & Policy Pages",
          subtitle = "Transparent policies explaining how Faizul Foundation handles information and help requests. All policies are editable by the administrator.",
        )
      }
    }

    items(policies, key = { it.policyKey }) { policy ->
      PageWidthContainer {
        Card(
          modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable { onOpenPolicy(policy.policyKey) }
            .testTag("legal_item_${policy.policyKey}"),
          colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
          border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
        ) {
          Row(
            modifier = Modifier
              .fillMaxWidth()
              .padding(16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
          ) {
            Icon(
              imageVector = Icons.Outlined.Description,
              contentDescription = null,
              tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
              modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
            )
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
              Text(
                text = policy.title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
              )
              Text(
                text = "Updated ${formatDate(policy.lastUpdatedMillis)}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              )
            }
            Icon(
              imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.KeyboardArrowRight,
              contentDescription = null,
              tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
            )
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

@Composable
fun LegalDetailScreen(
  policy: LegalPolicyEntity?,
  modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
  LazyColumn(
    modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 20.dp),
    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
  ) {
    if (policy == null) {
      item {
        PageWidthContainer {
          Text(
            text = "Policy document not found.",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
          )
        }
      }
    } else {
      item {
        PageWidthContainer {
          Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
          ) {
            OfficialFoundationLogo(size = 44.dp, cornerRadius = 8.dp)
            Column {
              Text(
                text = policy.title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium,
              )
              Text(
                text = "Last updated: ${formatDate(policy.lastUpdatedMillis)}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              )
            }
          }
        }
      }

      item {
        PageWidthContainer {
          Card(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
          ) {
            Text(
              text = policy.content,
              style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
              color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
              modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp),
            )
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

private fun formatDate(millis: Long): String {
  return DateFormat.getDateInstance(DateFormat.MEDIUM).format(Date(millis))
}

@Composable
fun AboutScreen(

    modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),

  ) {
    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        Row(
          verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
          horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
        ) {

          Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            SectionHeader(
              title = "About Us",
              subtitle = "Our purpose, values, and commitment to transparent service.",
            )
          }
        }
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        AboutCard(
          title = "Who We Are",
          body = siteContent.aboutWhoWeAre,
          icon = Icons.Outlined.Handshake,
        )
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        AboutCard(
          title = "Why Faizul Foundation Was Created",
          body = siteContent.aboutWhyCreated,
          icon = Icons.Outlined.FavoriteBorder,
        )
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        AboutCard(
          title = "Our Mission & Vision",
          body = "${siteContent.missionStatement}\n\n${siteContent.aboutVision}",
          icon = Icons.Outlined.Lightbulb,
        )
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        AboutCard(
          title = "Commitment to Honesty, Kindness & Transparency",
          body = siteContent.aboutCommitment,
          icon = Icons.Outlined.VerifiedUser,
          highlighted = true,
        )
      }
    }
  }
}

@Composable
private fun AboutCard(
  title: String,
  body: String,
  icon: ImageVector,
  highlighted: Boolean = false,
) {
  Card(
    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
      containerColor = if (highlighted) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer
      else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface,
    ),
    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
  ) {
    Column(
      modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
      verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
    ) {
      Row(
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
      ) {
        Icon(
          imageVector = icon,
          contentDescription = null,
          tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
          modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
        )
        Text(
          text = title,
          style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
          color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
        )
      }
      Text(
        text = body,
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
        color = if (highlighted) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer
        else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
      )
    }
  }
}

package com.example.ui.screens

import android.content.Intent

import androidx.compose.material.icons.outlined.Edit

private enum class AdminSectionTab(val label: String) {
  REQUESTS("Help Requests"),
  ACTIVITIES("Activities"),
  IMPACT("Impact"),
  CONTENT("Contact & Content"),
  POLICIES("Policies"),
  SECURITY("Security"),
}

@Composable
fun AdminScreen(
  authState: AdminAuthUiState,
  helpRequests: List<HelpRequestEntity>,
  activities: List<FoundationActivityEntity>,
  impactMetrics: ImpactMetricsEntity,
  contactConfig: ContactConfigEntity,
  siteContent: SiteContentEntity,
  legalPolicies: List<LegalPolicyEntity>,
  onGoogleSignInSuccess: () -> Unit,
  onGoogleSignInError: (String) -> Unit,
  onSignOut: () -> Unit,
  onSyncLocalToCloud: () -> Unit,
  onUpdateHelpRequest: (HelpRequestEntity, RequestStatus, String) -> Unit,
  onDeleteHelpRequest: (HelpRequestEntity) -> Unit,
  onSaveActivity: (Long?, String?, String, String, String, String, List<String>, List<Uri>) -> Unit,
  onDeleteActivity: (FoundationActivityEntity) -> Unit,
  onSaveImpact: (ImpactMetricsEntity) -> Unit,
  onSaveContact: (ContactConfigEntity) -> Unit,
  onSaveSiteContent: (SiteContentEntity) -> Unit,
  onSavePolicy: (LegalPolicyEntity) -> Unit,
  onPrepareAttachmentPreview: suspend (String, String) -> File?,
  onClearFeedback: () -> Unit,
  modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
  if (!authState.isAuthenticated || !authState.isAuthorizedAdmin) {
    AdminGoogleAuthGateScreen(
      authState = authState,
      onGoogleSignInSuccess = onGoogleSignInSuccess,
      onGoogleSignInError = onGoogleSignInError,
      onSignOut = onSignOut,
      modifier = modifier,
    )
    return
  }

  var activeTab by remember { mutableStateOf(AdminSectionTab.REQUESTS) }
  var selectedRequestForReview by remember { mutableStateOf<HelpRequestEntity?>(null) }
  var activityToEdit by remember { mutableStateOf<FoundationActivityEntity?>(null) }
  var showNewActivityDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

  LazyColumn(
    modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 20.dp),
    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
  ) {
    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        Row(
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
          horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
          verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        ) {
          Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(
              text = "Administrator Portal",
              style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium,
            )
            Text(
              text = "Cloud Firestore connected • ${helpRequests.size} private help requests",
              style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
              color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
            )
          }
          OutlinedButton(
            onClick = onSignOut,
            modifier = Modifier.testTag("admin_header_sign_out_btn"),
          ) {
            Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Outlined.Logout, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
            Text("Sign Out")
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (authState.feedbackMessage != null) {
      item {
        PageWidthContainer {
          Surface(
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer,
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
            border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
          ) {
            Row(
              modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
              horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
              Text(
                text = authState.feedbackMessage,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer,
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
              )
              TextButton(onClick = onClearFeedback) { Text("Dismiss") }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer {
        Row(
          modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
          horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        ) {
          AdminSectionTab.entries.forEach { tab ->
            FilterChip(
              selected = activeTab == tab,
              onClick = { activeTab = tab },
              label = { Text(tab.label) },
              modifier = Modifier.testTag("admin_tab_${tab.name.lowercase()}"),
            )
          }
        }
      }
    }

    when (activeTab) {
      AdminSectionTab.REQUESTS -> {
        if (helpRequests.isEmpty()) {
          item {
            PageWidthContainer {
              EmptySectionCard(
                icon = Icons.Outlined.AdminPanelSettings,
                title = "No Help Requests Yet",
                message = "Private help requests submitted via the public form will sync from Cloud Firestore here for your review.",
              )
            }
          }
        } else {
          items(helpRequests, key = { it.referenceCode }) { request ->
            PageWidthContainer {
              AdminHelpRequestCard(
                request = request,
                onReviewClick = { selectedRequestForReview = request },
              )
            }
          }
        }
      }

      AdminSectionTab.ACTIVITIES -> {
        item {
          PageWidthContainer {
            Button(
              onClick = {
                activityToEdit = null
                showNewActivityDialog = true
              },
              modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(48.dp)
                .testTag("admin_add_activity_btn"),
            ) {
              Icon(Icons.Outlined.Add, contentDescription = null)
              Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
              Text("Add Foundation Activity")
            }
          }
        }

        if (activities.isEmpty()) {
          item {
            PageWidthContainer {
              EmptySectionCard(
                icon = Icons.Outlined.Image,
                title = "No Published Activities",
                message = "Add genuine foundation activities with title, description, date, location, and optional photos.",
              )
            }
          }
        } else {
          items(activities, key = { it.cloudId.ifBlank { it.id.toString() } }) { activity ->
            PageWidthContainer {
              Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
                border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
              ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                  Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                  ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                      Text(activity.title, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
                      Text(
                        "${activity.activityDate} • ${activity.location}",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                      )
                    }
                    Row {
                      IconButton(onClick = {
                        activityToEdit = activity
                        showNewActivityDialog = true
                      }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Outlined.Edit, contentDescription = "Edit activity")
                      }
                      IconButton(onClick = { onDeleteActivity(activity) }) {
                        Icon(
                          Icons.Outlined.Delete,
                          contentDescription = "Delete activity",
                          tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
                        )
                      }
                    }
                  }
                  Text(
                    text = activity.description,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  )
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }

      AdminSectionTab.IMPACT -> {
        item {
          PageWidthContainer {
            AdminImpactEditor(
              impact = impactMetrics,
              onSaveImpact = onSaveImpact,
            )
          }
        }
      }

      AdminSectionTab.CONTENT -> {
        item {
          PageWidthContainer {
            AdminContactAndContentEditor(
              contactConfig = contactConfig,
              siteContent = siteContent,
              onSaveContact = onSaveContact,
              onSaveSiteContent = onSaveSiteContent,
            )
          }
        }
      }

      AdminSectionTab.POLICIES -> {
        item {
          PageWidthContainer {
            AdminPolicyEditor(
              policies = legalPolicies,
              onSavePolicy = onSavePolicy,
            )
          }
        }
      }

      AdminSectionTab.SECURITY -> {
        item {
          PageWidthContainer {
            AdminSecurityEditor(
              signedInEmail = authState.signedInEmail,
              onSyncLocalToCloud = onSyncLocalToCloud,
              onSignOutAdmin = onSignOut,
            )
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  selectedRequestForReview?.let { request ->
    HelpRequestReviewDialog(
      request = request,
      onPrepareAttachmentPreview = onPrepareAttachmentPreview,
      onDismiss = { selectedRequestForReview = null },
      onSave = { status, notes ->
        onUpdateHelpRequest(request, status, notes)
        selectedRequestForReview = null
      },
      onDelete = {
        onDeleteHelpRequest(request)
        selectedRequestForReview = null
      },
    )
  }

  if (showNewActivityDialog) {
    ActivityEditorDialog(
      existing = activityToEdit,
      onDismiss = { showNewActivityDialog = false },
      onSave = { title, description, date, location, keptPaths, newUris ->
        onSaveActivity(
          activityToEdit?.id,
          activityToEdit?.cloudId?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() },
          title,
          description,
          date,
          location,
          keptPaths,
          newUris,
        )
        showNewActivityDialog = false
      },
    )
  }
}

@Composable
private fun AdminGoogleAuthGateScreen(
  authState: AdminAuthUiState,
  onGoogleSignInSuccess: () -> Unit,
  onGoogleSignInError: (String) -> Unit,
  onSignOut: () -> Unit,
  modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
  val context = LocalContext.current
  val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
  val credentialManager = remember(context) { CredentialManager.create(context) }
  var isSigningIn by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

  LaunchedEffect(authState.usesCloudAuth) {
    if (authState.usesCloudAuth && !authState.isAuthenticated) {
      GoogleAuthHelper.attemptAutoSignIn(
        context = context,
        credentialManager = credentialManager,
        onAuthSuccess = onGoogleSignInSuccess,
        onUnauthenticated = {},
        scope = coroutineScope,
      )
    }
  }

  LazyColumn(
    modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize(),
    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 24.dp),
    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
  ) {
    item {
      PageWidthContainer(maxWidth = 520.dp) {
        InfoNoticeCard(
          title = "Restricted Administrator Area",
          body = "Public visitors cannot read or browse private help requests. Sign in with the authorized Faizul Foundation administrator Google account to review help requests and publish cloud updates.",
          icon = Icons.Outlined.Lock,
        )
      }
    }

    item {
      PageWidthContainer(maxWidth = 520.dp) {
        Card(
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
          colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
          border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
        ) {
          Column(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
          ) {
            Text(
              text = if (authState.isAuthenticated && !authState.isAuthorizedAdmin)
                "Administrator Authorization Required"
              else
                "Administrator Google Sign-In",
              style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
            )

            if (authState.isAuthenticated && !authState.isAuthorizedAdmin) {
              Text(
                text = "Signed in as ${authState.signedInEmail ?: "visitor"}. This Google account does not have administrator privileges under Firebase Security Rules.",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
              )
              OutlinedButton(
                onClick = onSignOut,
                modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .height(48.dp)
                  .testTag("admin_switch_account_btn"),
              ) {
                Text("Sign Out & Switch Google Account")
              }
            } else {
              Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
              ) {
                Icon(
                  imageVector = Icons.Outlined.VerifiedUser,
                  contentDescription = null,
                  tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
                  modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp),
                )
                Text(
                  text = "Protected by Firebase Authentication & Firestore Rules",
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                )
              }

              if (authState.authError != null) {
                Text(
                  text = authState.authError,
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
                )
              }

              Button(
                onClick = {
                  isSigningIn = true
                  GoogleAuthHelper.onGoogleSignInClicked(
                    context = context,
                    credentialManager = credentialManager,
                    onAuthSuccess = {
                      isSigningIn = false
                      onGoogleSignInSuccess()
                    },
                    onAuthError = { msg ->
                      isSigningIn = false
                      onGoogleSignInError(msg)
                    },
                    scope = coroutineScope,
                    onAuthCancelled = { isSigningIn = false },
                  )
                },
                enabled = !isSigningIn && !authState.isProcessing,
                modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .height(50.dp)
                  .testTag("admin_google_sign_in_btn"),
              ) {
                if (isSigningIn || authState.isProcessing) {
                  CircularProgressIndicator(
                    modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary,
                    strokeWidth = 2.dp,
                  )
                } else {
                  Text("Sign in with Google", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

@Composable
private fun AdminHelpRequestCard(
  request: HelpRequestEntity,
  onReviewClick: () -> Unit,
) {
  Card(
    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
  ) {
    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
      Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
      ) {
        Text(
          text = "${request.referenceCode} • ${request.category}",
          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
          color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
          fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        )
        Surface(
          color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer,
          shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
        ) {
          Text(
            text = request.status,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
          )
        }
      }
      Text(request.fullName, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
      Text(
        text = "${request.mobileNumber} • ${request.location}",
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
      )
      Text(
        text = request.description,
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
        maxLines = 3,
      )
      Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
      ) {
        Text(
          text = DateFormat.getDateTimeInstance(DateFormat.SHORT, DateFormat.SHORT)
            .format(Date(request.createdAtMillis)),
          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
          color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
        )
        OutlinedButton(
          onClick = onReviewClick,
          modifier = Modifier.testTag("admin_review_req_${request.referenceCode}"),
        ) {
          Text("Review & Update")
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
private fun HelpRequestReviewDialog(
  request: HelpRequestEntity,
  onPrepareAttachmentPreview: suspend (String, String) -> File?,
  onDismiss: () -> Unit,
  onSave: (RequestStatus, String) -> Unit,
  onDelete: () -> Unit,
) {
  val context = LocalContext.current
  val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
  var statusExpanded by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
  var selectedStatus by remember {
    mutableStateOf(
      RequestStatus.entries.firstOrNull { it.displayName == request.status }
        ?: RequestStatus.PENDING_REVIEW
    )
  }
  var adminNotes by remember { mutableStateOf(request.adminNotes) }

  val imagePreviewModel = remember(request.attachmentPath, request.attachmentDataUrl) {
    val localFile = request.attachmentPath?.let { File(it) }?.takeIf { it.exists() }
    localFile ?: request.attachmentDataUrl?.let { AttachmentStorage.toCoilModel(it) }
  }

  AlertDialog(
    onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
    title = { Text("Review ${request.referenceCode}") },
    text = {
      Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
        Text("Applicant: ${request.fullName}", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall)
        Text("Mobile: ${request.mobileNumber}", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall)
        Text("Location: ${request.location}", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall)
        Text("Category: ${request.category}", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall)
        Text(request.description, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)

        if (request.attachmentMimeType?.startsWith("image/") == true && imagePreviewModel != null) {
          AsyncImage(
            model = imagePreviewModel,
            contentDescription = "Applicant attachment",
            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
            modifier = Modifier
              .fillMaxWidth()
              .height(140.dp)
              .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)),
          )
        } else if (!request.attachmentPath.isNullOrBlank() || !request.attachmentDataUrl.isNullOrBlank()) {
          OutlinedButton(
            onClick = {
              coroutineScope.launch {
                val file = request.attachmentPath?.let { File(it) }?.takeIf { it.exists() }
                  ?: request.attachmentDataUrl?.let {
                    onPrepareAttachmentPreview(it, request.attachmentName ?: "${request.referenceCode}.dat")
                  }
                if (file != null && file.exists()) {
                  val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                    context,
                    "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
                    file,
                  )
                  val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                    setDataAndType(uri, request.attachmentMimeType ?: "*/*")
                    addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                  }
                  runCatching { context.startActivity(intent) }
                }
              }
            },
          ) {
            Icon(Icons.Outlined.AttachFile, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
            Text(request.attachmentName ?: "Open Attachment")
          }
        }

        ExposedDropdownMenuBox(
          expanded = statusExpanded,
          onExpandedChange = { statusExpanded = !statusExpanded },
        ) {
          OutlinedTextField(
            value = selectedStatus.displayName,
            onValueChange = {},
            readOnly = true,
            label = { Text("Status") },
            trailingIcon = { ExposedDropdownMenuDefaults.TrailingIcon(expanded = statusExpanded) },
            modifier = Modifier
              .fillMaxWidth()
              .menuAnchor(MenuAnchorType.PrimaryNotEditable),
          )
          ExposedDropdownMenu(
            expanded = statusExpanded,
            onDismissRequest = { statusExpanded = false },
          ) {
            RequestStatus.entries.forEach { status ->
              DropdownMenuItem(
                text = { Text(status.displayName) },
                onClick = {
                  selectedStatus = status
                  statusExpanded = false
                },
              )
            }
          }
        }

        OutlinedTextField(
          value = adminNotes,
          onValueChange = { adminNotes = it },
          label = { Text("Private Administrator Notes") },
          minLines = 2,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
      }
    },
    confirmButton = {
      Button(onClick = { onSave(selectedStatus, adminNotes) }) {
        Text("Save Review")
      }
    },
    dismissButton = {
      Row {
        TextButton(onClick = onDelete) {
          Text("Delete", color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error)
        }
        TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) {
          Text("Cancel")
        }
      }
    },
  )
}

@Composable
private fun ActivityEditorDialog(
  existing: FoundationActivityEntity?,
  onDismiss: () -> Unit,
  onSave: (String, String, String, String, List<String>, List<Uri>) -> Unit,
) {
  var title by remember { mutableStateOf(existing?.title ?: "") }
  var description by remember { mutableStateOf(existing?.description ?: "") }
  var date by remember { mutableStateOf(existing?.activityDate ?: "") }
  var location by remember { mutableStateOf(existing?.location ?: "") }
  val keptPaths = remember {
    mutableStateListOf<String>().apply {
      addAll(existing?.photoPaths() ?: emptyList())
    }
  }
  val newPhotoUris = remember { mutableStateListOf<Uri>() }
  var validationError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

  val photoPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
    contract = ActivityResultContracts.PickMultipleVisualMedia(maxItems = 5),
  ) { uris ->
    newPhotoUris.addAll(uris)
  }

  AlertDialog(
    onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
    title = { Text(if (existing == null) "Add Foundation Activity" else "Edit Activity") },
    text = {
      Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = title,
          onValueChange = { title = it; validationError = null },
          label = { Text("Activity Title *") },
          singleLine = true,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_act_title_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = date,
          onValueChange = { date = it; validationError = null },
          label = { Text("Date (e.g., 15 Nov 2026) *") },
          singleLine = true,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_act_date_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = location,
          onValueChange = { location = it; validationError = null },
          label = { Text("Location *") },
          singleLine = true,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_act_location_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = description,
          onValueChange = { description = it; validationError = null },
          label = { Text("Description *") },
          minLines = 3,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_act_desc_input"),
        )
        OutlinedButton(
          onClick = {
            photoPicker.launch(
              PickVisualMediaRequest(ActivityResultContracts.PickVisualMedia.ImageOnly)
            )
          },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        ) {
          Icon(Icons.Outlined.Image, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
          Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
          Text("Add Photos (${keptPaths.size + newPhotoUris.size} selected)")
        }
        if (validationError != null) {
          Text(
            text = validationError!!,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
          )
        }
      }
    },
    confirmButton = {
      Button(
        onClick = {
          if (title.isBlank() || date.isBlank() || location.isBlank() || description.isBlank()) {
            validationError = "Please complete all required fields."
          } else {
            onSave(title, description, date, location, keptPaths.toList(), newPhotoUris.toList())
          }
        },
        modifier = Modifier.testTag("admin_act_save_btn"),
      ) {
        Text("Publish")
      }
    },
    dismissButton = {
      TextButton(onClick = onDismiss) { Text("Cancel") }
    },
  )
}


==========================================================================================

package com.example.ui.screens

@Composable
fun AdminImpactEditor(
  impact: ImpactMetricsEntity,
  onSaveImpact: (ImpactMetricsEntity) -> Unit,
) {
  var peopleVerified by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.peopleHelped != null) }
  var peopleValue by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.peopleHelped?.toString() ?: "") }

  var studentsVerified by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.studentsSupported != null) }
  var studentsValue by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.studentsSupported?.toString() ?: "") }

  var familiesVerified by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.familiesSupported != null) }
  var familiesValue by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.familiesSupported?.toString() ?: "") }

  var madrasasVerified by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.madrasasSupported != null) }
  var madrasasValue by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.madrasasSupported?.toString() ?: "") }

  var masjidsVerified by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.masjidsSupported != null) }
  var masjidsValue by remember(impact) { mutableStateOf(impact.masjidsSupported?.toString() ?: "") }

  Card(
    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
  ) {
    Column(
      modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
      verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
    ) {
      Text("Verified Impact Numbers", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge)
      Text(
        text = "Leave toggle off to display \"Data Coming Soon\". Only turn on and enter figures that are officially verified.",
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
      )

      ImpactFieldRow(
        label = "Total People Helped",
        verified = peopleVerified,
        value = peopleValue,
        onToggle = { peopleVerified = it },
        onValueChange = { peopleValue = it.filter(Char::isDigit) },
        testTagPrefix = "impact_people",
      )
      ImpactFieldRow(
        label = "Students Supported",
        verified = studentsVerified,
        value = studentsValue,
        onToggle = { studentsVerified = it },
        onValueChange = { studentsValue = it.filter(Char::isDigit) },
        testTagPrefix = "impact_students",
      )
      ImpactFieldRow(
        label = "Families Supported",
        verified = familiesVerified,
        value = familiesValue,
        onToggle = { familiesVerified = it },
        onValueChange = { familiesValue = it.filter(Char::isDigit) },
        testTagPrefix = "impact_families",
      )
      ImpactFieldRow(
        label = "Madrasas Supported",
        verified = madrasasVerified,
        value = madrasasValue,
        onToggle = { madrasasVerified = it },
        onValueChange = { madrasasValue = it.filter(Char::isDigit) },
        testTagPrefix = "impact_madrasas",
      )
      ImpactFieldRow(
        label = "Masjids Supported",
        verified = masjidsVerified,
        value = masjidsValue,
        onToggle = { masjidsVerified = it },
        onValueChange = { masjidsValue = it.filter(Char::isDigit) },
        testTagPrefix = "impact_masjids",
      )

      Button(
        onClick = {
          onSaveImpact(
            ImpactMetricsEntity(
              id = 1,
              peopleHelped = if (peopleVerified) peopleValue.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 else null,
              studentsSupported = if (studentsVerified) studentsValue.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 else null,
              familiesSupported = if (familiesVerified) familiesValue.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 else null,
              madrasasSupported = if (madrasasVerified) madrasasValue.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 else null,
              masjidsSupported = if (masjidsVerified) masjidsValue.toIntOrNull() ?: 0 else null,
            )
          )
        },
        modifier = Modifier
          .fillMaxWidth()
          .height(48.dp)
          .testTag("admin_save_impact_btn"),
      ) {
        Text("Save Verified Impact")
      }
    }
  }
}

@Composable
private fun ImpactFieldRow(
  label: String,
  verified: Boolean,
  value: String,
  onToggle: (Boolean) -> Unit,
  onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
  testTagPrefix: String,
) {
  Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
    Row(
      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
      horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
      verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
    ) {
      Text(label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall)
      Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Text(
          text = if (verified) "Published" else "Coming Soon",
          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
          color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
        Switch(
          checked = verified,
          onCheckedChange = onToggle,
          modifier = Modifier.testTag("${testTagPrefix}_switch"),
        )
      }
    }
    if (verified) {
      OutlinedTextField(
        value = value,
        onValueChange = onValueChange,
        label = { Text("Verified count for $label") },
        keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
        singleLine = true,
        modifier = Modifier
          .fillMaxWidth()
          .testTag("${testTagPrefix}_input"),
      )
    }
  }
}

@Composable
fun AdminContactAndContentEditor(
  contactConfig: ContactConfigEntity,
  siteContent: SiteContentEntity,
  onSaveContact: (ContactConfigEntity) -> Unit,
  onSaveSiteContent: (SiteContentEntity) -> Unit,
) {
  var contactPerson by remember(contactConfig) { mutableStateOf(contactConfig.contactPerson) }
  var mobileNumber by remember(contactConfig) { mutableStateOf(contactConfig.mobileNumber) }
  var emailAddress by remember(contactConfig) { mutableStateOf(contactConfig.emailAddress) }
  var address by remember(contactConfig) { mutableStateOf(contactConfig.address) }
  var whatsappNumber by remember(contactConfig) { mutableStateOf(contactConfig.whatsappNumber) }

  var missionStatement by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.missionStatement) }
  var homeIntro by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.homeIntroduction) }
  var whoWeAre by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.aboutWhoWeAre) }
  var whyCreated by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.aboutWhyCreated) }
  var vision by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.aboutVision) }
  var commitment by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.aboutCommitment) }
  var supportMessage by remember(siteContent) { mutableStateOf(siteContent.supportUsMessage) }

  Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)) {
    Card(
      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
      colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
      border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
    ) {
      Column(
        modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
      ) {
        Text("Contact Details", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge)
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = contactPerson,
          onValueChange = { contactPerson = it },
          label = { Text("Contact Person") },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_contact_person_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = mobileNumber,
          onValueChange = { mobileNumber = it },
          label = { Text("Mobile Number") },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_contact_mobile_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = emailAddress,
          onValueChange = { emailAddress = it },
          label = { Text("Email Address") },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_contact_email_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = address,
          onValueChange = { address = it },
          label = { Text("Address") },
          minLines = 2,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_contact_address_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = whatsappNumber,
          onValueChange = { whatsappNumber = it },
          label = { Text("WhatsApp Number (with country code, optional)") },
          placeholder = { Text("Leave blank to hide WhatsApp button") },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_contact_whatsapp_input"),
        )
        Button(
          onClick = {
            onSaveContact(
              ContactConfigEntity(
                id = 1,
                contactPerson = contactPerson.trim(),
                mobileNumber = mobileNumber.trim(),
                emailAddress = emailAddress.trim(),
                address = address.trim(),
                whatsappNumber = whatsappNumber.trim(),
              )
            )
          },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(48.dp).testTag("admin_save_contact_btn"),
        ) {
          Text("Save Contact Details")
        }
      }
    }

    Card(
      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
      colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
      border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
    ) {
      Column(
        modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
      ) {
        Text("Website & Foundation Content", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge)
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = missionStatement,
          onValueChange = { missionStatement = it },
          label = { Text("Our Mission Statement") },
          minLines = 2,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_mission_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = homeIntro,
          onValueChange = { homeIntro = it },
          label = { Text("Home Introduction") },
          minLines = 3,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("admin_home_intro_input"),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = whoWeAre,
          onValueChange = { whoWeAre = it },
          label = { Text("About Us: Who We Are") },
          minLines = 3,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = whyCreated,
          onValueChange = { whyCreated = it },
          label = { Text("About Us: Why Faizul Foundation Was Created") },
          minLines = 3,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = vision,
          onValueChange = { vision = it },
          label = { Text("About Us: Vision") },
          minLines = 3,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = commitment,
          onValueChange = { commitment = it },
          label = { Text("About Us: Commitment & Transparency") },
          minLines = 3,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
        OutlinedTextField(
          value = supportMessage,
          onValueChange = { supportMessage = it },
          label = { Text("Support Us Notice") },
          minLines = 2,
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        )
        Button(
          onClick = {
            onSaveSiteContent(
              SiteContentEntity(
                id = 1,
                missionStatement = missionStatement.trim(),
                homeIntroduction = homeIntro.trim(),
                aboutWhoWeAre = whoWeAre.trim(),
                aboutWhyCreated = whyCreated.trim(),
                aboutVision = vision.trim(),
                aboutCommitment = commitment.trim(),
                supportUsMessage = supportMessage.trim(),
              )
            )
          },
          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(48.dp).testTag("admin_save_content_btn"),
        ) {
          Text("Save Website Content")
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun AdminPolicyEditor(
  policies: List<LegalPolicyEntity>,
  onSavePolicy: (LegalPolicyEntity) -> Unit,
) {
  var selectedKey by remember(policies) {
    mutableStateOf(policies.firstOrNull()?.policyKey ?: "")
  }
  val selectedPolicy = policies.find { it.policyKey == selectedKey } ?: policies.firstOrNull()
  var dropdownExpanded by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
  var editedContent by remember(selectedPolicy?.policyKey) {
    mutableStateOf(selectedPolicy?.content ?: "")
  }

  LaunchedEffect(selectedPolicy?.content) {
    if (selectedPolicy != null) {
      editedContent = selectedPolicy.content
    }
  }

  Card(
    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
  ) {
    Column(
      modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
      verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
    ) {
      Text("Edit Legal & Policy Pages", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge)
      if (selectedPolicy != null) {
        ExposedDropdownMenuBox(
          expanded = dropdownExpanded,
          onExpandedChange = { dropdownExpanded = !dropdownExpanded },
        ) {
          OutlinedTextField(
            value = selectedPolicy.title,
            onValueChange = {},
            readOnly = true,
            label = { Text("Select Policy Page") },
            trailingIcon = {
              ExposedDropdownMenuDefaults.TrailingIcon(expanded = dropdownExpanded)
            },
            modifier = Modifier
              .fillMaxWidth()
              .menuAnchor(MenuAnchorType.PrimaryNotEditable)
              .testTag("admin_policy_dropdown"),
          )
          ExposedDropdownMenu(
            expanded = dropdownExpanded,
            onDismissRequest = { dropdownExpanded = false },
          ) {
            policies.forEach { policy ->
              DropdownMenuItem(
                text = { Text(policy.title) },
                onClick = {
                  selectedKey = policy.policyKey
                  editedContent = policy.content
                  dropdownExpanded = false
                },
              )
            }
          }
        }

        OutlinedTextField(
          value = editedContent,
          onValueChange = { editedContent = it },
          label = { Text("Policy Content") },
          minLines = 10,
          modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .testTag("admin_policy_content_input"),
        )

        Button(
          onClick = {
            onSavePolicy(selectedPolicy.copy(content = editedContent.trim()))
          },
          modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(48.dp)
            .testTag("admin_save_policy_btn"),
        ) {
          Text("Save Policy Changes")
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

@Composable
fun AdminSecurityEditor(
  signedInEmail: String?,
  onSyncLocalToCloud: () -> Unit,
  onSignOutAdmin: () -> Unit,
) {
  Card(
    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
    border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant),
  ) {
    Column(
      modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp),
      verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp),
    ) {
      Text("Administrator Security & Cloud Sync", style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge)
      Text(

        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,

      OutlinedTextField(
        value = signedInEmail ?: "Verified Administrator",

        readOnly = true,

      )
      Button(

FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/FoundationRepositoryTest.kt
==========================================================================================
package com.example

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.data.local.AdminSecurityManager
import com.example.data.local.AttachmentStorage
import com.example.data.local.FaizulDatabase
import com.example.data.local.FoundationRepository
import com.example.data.local.HelpCategory
import com.example.data.local.ImpactMetricsEntity
import com.example.data.local.RequestStatus
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class FoundationRepositoryTest {

  private lateinit var context: Context
  private lateinit var database: FaizulDatabase
  private lateinit var repository: FoundationRepository

  @Before
  fun setUp() {
    context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
    database = Room.inMemoryDatabaseBuilder(context, FaizulDatabase::class.java)
      .allowMainThreadQueries()
      .build()
    repository = FoundationRepository(
      database = database,
      securityManager = AdminSecurityManager(context),
      attachmentStorage = AttachmentStorage(context),
    )
  }

  @After
  fun tearDown() {
    database.close()
  }

  @Test
  fun ensureSeedData_keepsImpactUnverifiedAndActivitiesEmpty() = runBlocking {
    repository.ensureSeedData()

    val impact = repository.impactMetrics.first()
    assertNull(impact.peopleHelped)
    assertNull(impact.studentsSupported)
    assertNull(impact.familiesSupported)
    assertNull(impact.madrasasSupported)
    assertNull(impact.masjidsSupported)

    val activities = repository.activities.first()
    assertTrue(activities.isEmpty())

    val siteContent = repository.siteContent.first()
    assertEquals(
      FoundationRepository.DEFAULT_MISSION_STATEMENT,
      siteContent.missionStatement,
    )
    assertEquals(
      "We are currently preparing our foundation's support system. Donations are not being accepted at this time.",
      siteContent.supportUsMessage,
    )

    val contact = repository.contactConfig.first()
    assertEquals(FoundationRepository.DEFAULT_HELPLINE, contact.mobileNumber)
    assertEquals(FoundationRepository.DEFAULT_EMAIL, contact.emailAddress)
    assertEquals(FoundationRepository.DEFAULT_HELPLINE, contact.whatsappNumber)

    val policies = repository.legalPolicies.first()
    assertEquals(6, policies.size)
  }

  @Test
  fun adminPassphrase_configureVerifyAndChange_succeeds() = runBlocking {
    val uniquePass = "FaizulAdmin#2026"
    val nextPass = "FaizulAdmin#2027"
    if (!repository.isAdminConfigured.first()) {
      repository.configureInitialAdminPassphrase(uniquePass)
    }
    assertTrue(repository.verifyAdminPassphrase(uniquePass))
    assertFalse(repository.verifyAdminPassphrase("wrong-passphrase"))

    assertTrue(repository.changeAdminPassphrase(uniquePass, nextPass))
    assertFalse(repository.verifyAdminPassphrase(uniquePass))
    assertTrue(repository.verifyAdminPassphrase(nextPass))
  }

  @Test
  fun submitHelpRequest_storesPrivatelyAndRespectsCooldownAndAdminReview() = runBlocking {
    repository.ensureSeedData()

    val firstResult = repository.submitHelpRequest(
      fullName = "Amina Begum",
      mobileNumber = "9876543210",
      location = "Lucknow, UP",
      category = HelpCategory.EDUCATION_SUPPORT,
      description = "Need assistance for school textbooks and uniform for two children.",
      attachmentUri = null,
      consentAccepted = true,
    )
    assertTrue(firstResult.isSuccess)

    // Rapid second submission is blocked by spam cooldown
    val rapidSecond = repository.submitHelpRequest(
      fullName = "Duplicate Caller",
      mobileNumber = "9876543210",
      location = "Lucknow, UP",
      category = HelpCategory.OTHER_ASSISTANCE,
      description = "Repeated immediate submission should be rate limited.",
      attachmentUri = null,
      consentAccepted = true,
    )
    assertTrue(rapidSecond.isFailure)

    val stored = repository.observeHelpRequestsForAdmin().first()
    assertEquals(1, stored.size)
    assertEquals("Amina Begum", stored.first().fullName)
    assertEquals(RequestStatus.PENDING_REVIEW.displayName, stored.first().status)

    repository.updateHelpRequestReview(
      request = stored.first(),
      newStatus = RequestStatus.UNDER_REVIEW,
      adminNotes = "Contacted family for verification.",
    )
    val updated = repository.observeHelpRequestsForAdmin().first().first()
    assertEquals(RequestStatus.UNDER_REVIEW.displayName, updated.status)
    assertEquals("Contacted family for verification.", updated.adminNotes)

    repository.updateImpactMetrics(
      ImpactMetricsEntity(
        peopleHelped = 12,
        studentsSupported = 5,
        familiesSupported = 3,
        madrasasSupported = null,
        masjidsSupported = null,
      )
    )
    val impact = repository.impactMetrics.first()
    assertEquals(12, impact.peopleHelped)
    assertNull(impact.madrasasSupported)
  }
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/ui/components/IntentActions.kt
==========================================================================================
package com.example.ui.components

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.widget.Toast

object IntentActions {
  fun normalizeWhatsAppDigits(rawNumber: String): String {
    val digits = rawNumber.filter { it.isDigit() }
    return if (digits.length == 10) "91$digits" else digits
  }

  fun dialPhone(context: Context, rawNumber: String) {
    val cleaned = rawNumber.filter { it.isDigit() || it == '+' }
    if (cleaned.isBlank()) return
    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_DIAL, Uri.parse("tel:$cleaned"))
    launchSafely(context, intent, "Unable to open phone dialler")
  }

  fun openWhatsApp(context: Context, rawNumber: String) {
    val waDigits = normalizeWhatsAppDigits(rawNumber)
    if (waDigits.length < 10) return
    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://wa.me/$waDigits"))
    launchSafely(context, intent, "Unable to open WhatsApp")
  }

  fun composeEmail(context: Context, email: String) {
    val trimmed = email.trim()
    if (!trimmed.contains("@")) return
    val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SENDTO, Uri.parse("mailto:${Uri.encode(trimmed)}"))
    launchSafely(context, intent, "Unable to open email app")
  }

  private fun launchSafely(context: Context, intent: Intent, fallbackMsg: String) {
    runCatching {
      context.startActivity(intent)
    }.onFailure {
      Toast.makeText(context, fallbackMsg, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }
  }
}

package com.example

import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsDisplayed

import com.example.data.local.FoundationRepository
import com.example.ui.components.IntentActions
import com.example.ui.screens.HomeScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.RequestHelpScreen

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36], qualifiers = "w411dp-h915dp")
class HomepageAndRequestHelpUiTest {

  @get:Rule
  val composeRule = createComposeRule()

  @Test
  fun homepage_displaysSliderMissionAreasRequestHelpAndContactSection() {
    var requestHelpClicked = false
    val state = PublicContentState(
      siteContent = FoundationRepository.defaultSiteContent(),
      impactMetrics = FoundationRepository.defaultImpact(),
      contactConfig = FoundationRepository.defaultContact(),
    )

    composeRule.setContent {
      FaizulFoundationTheme(darkTheme = false) {
        HomeScreen(
          contentState = state,
          onNavigateRequestHelp = { requestHelpClicked = true },
          onNavigateOurWork = {},
          onNavigateAbout = {},
          onNavigateSupportUs = {},
          onNavigateContactUs = {},
          onNavigateLegal = {},
          onNavigateLegalIndex = {},
          onNavigateAdmin = {},
        )
      }
    }

    // Slider & initial caption + 4 indicators
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("homepage_image_slider").assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Community Service & Team Planning").assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("slider_indicator_0").assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("slider_indicator_1").assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("slider_indicator_2").assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("slider_indicator_3").assertIsDisplayed()

    // Tap slide 2 indicator and confirm caption updates
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("slider_indicator_1").performClick()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Madrasa & Islamic Education Project").assertIsDisplayed()

    // Scroll to Foundation name, mission, and REQUEST HELP button
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_lazy_column")
      .performScrollToNode(hasTestTag("home_request_help_button"))
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("FAIZUL FOUNDATION").assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("\"${FoundationRepository.DEFAULT_MISSION_STATEMENT}\"")
      .assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_request_help_button").performScrollTo().performClick()
    assertTrue(requestHelpClicked)

    // Scroll to main areas of work
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_lazy_column")
      .performScrollToNode(hasText("Providing humanitarian assistance."))
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Helping poor and needy families.").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Supporting Muslim students.").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Helping Madrasas and Masjids.").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Supporting Islamic education.").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithText("Providing humanitarian assistance.").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()

    // Scroll to homepage Contact Us section
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_lazy_column")
      .performScrollToNode(hasTestTag("home_contact_email_us_btn"))
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_contact_call_us_btn").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_contact_whatsapp_btn").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_contact_email_us_btn").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()

    // Scroll to Footer with Contact Us buttons
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("home_lazy_column")
      .performScrollToNode(hasTestTag("footer_email_us_btn"))
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("footer_call_us_btn").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("footer_whatsapp_btn").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("footer_email_us_btn").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
  }

  @Test
  fun whatsappDigitsNormalization_prependsCountryCodeForTenDigits() {
    assertEquals("917983035055", IntentActions.normalizeWhatsAppDigits("7983035055"))
    assertEquals("917983035055", IntentActions.normalizeWhatsAppDigits("+91 79830 35055"))
  }

  @Test
  fun requestHelpScreen_rendersFormAndConfirmationStateWithoutLogin() {
    var submitted = false
    composeRule.setContent {
      FaizulFoundationTheme(darkTheme = false) {
        RequestHelpScreen(
          formState = RequestHelpFormState(
            fullName = "Zaid Khan",
            mobileNumber = "7983035055",
            location = "Rampur, UP",
            description = "Need books and tuition support for two students.",
            consentAccepted = true,
            spamLeft = 3,
            spamRight = 4,
            spamAnswer = "7",
          ),
          onFullNameChange = {},
          onMobileChange = {},
          onLocationChange = {},
          onCategorySelected = {},
          onDescriptionChange = {},
          onAttachmentSelected = { _, _ -> },
          onClearAttachment = {},
          onConsentChanged = {},
          onSpamAnswerChange = {},
          onRefreshSpamChallenge = {},
          onSubmitClick = { submitted = true },
          onStartNewRequest = {},
          onOpenPrivacyPolicy = {},
        )
      }
    }

    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("request_full_name_input").assertExists()
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("request_help_lazy_column")
      .performScrollToNode(hasTestTag("request_submit_button"))
    composeRule.onNodeWithTag("request_submit_button").performScrollTo().performClick()
    assertTrue(submitted)
  }
}
