FILE: firestore.rules
==========================================================================================
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {

    // Global Safety Net: default-deny catch-all
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // --- GLOBAL HARDENED PRIMITIVES ---
    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    // Bootstrapped Foundation Administrator (verified email claim required to prevent email spoofing)
    function isBootstrappedAdmin() {
      return isSignedIn() &&
             request.auth.token.email == 'faizul8057@gmail.com' &&
             request.auth.token.email_verified == true;
    }

    // Used only on single-document operations (get/create/update/delete), never inside allow list
    function isAdmin() {
      return isBootstrappedAdmin() ||
             (isSignedIn() && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/admins/$(request.auth.uid)));
    }

    // --- COLLECTION VALIDATION HELPERS ---
    function isValidHelpCategory(cat) {
      return cat in [
        'Financial Assistance',
        'Education Support',
        'Madrasa Support',
        'Masjid Support',
        'Food and Basic Needs',
        'Other Assistance'
      ];
    }

    function isValidRequestStatus(status) {
      return status in [
        'Pending Review',
        'Under Review',
        'Verified',
        'Assisted',
        'Closed'
      ];
    }

    function isValidHelpRequest(data) {
      return data.referenceCode is string && data.referenceCode.size() >= 2 && data.referenceCode.size() <= 32 &&
             data.fullName is string && data.fullName.size() >= 2 && data.fullName.size() <= 120 &&
             data.mobileNumber is string && data.mobileNumber.size() >= 10 && data.mobileNumber.size() <= 20 &&
             data.location is string && data.location.size() >= 3 && data.location.size() <= 200 &&
             data.category is string && isValidHelpCategory(data.category) &&
             data.description is string && data.description.size() >= 20 && data.description.size() <= 3000 &&
             (!('attachmentName' in data) || data.attachmentName == null || (data.attachmentName is string && data.attachmentName.size() <= 150)) &&
             (!('attachmentMimeType' in data) || data.attachmentMimeType == null || (data.attachmentMimeType is string && data.attachmentMimeType.size() <= 100)) &&
             (!('attachmentDataUrl' in data) || data.attachmentDataUrl == null || (data.attachmentDataUrl is string && data.attachmentDataUrl.size() <= 350000)) &&
             data.consentAccepted is bool && data.consentAccepted == true &&
             data.status is string && isValidRequestStatus(data.status) &&
             data.adminNotes is string && data.adminNotes.size() <= 2000;
    }

    function isValidActivity(data) {
      return data.title is string && data.title.size() >= 2 && data.title.size() <= 200 &&
             data.description is string && data.description.size() >= 5 && data.description.size() <= 4000 &&
             data.activityDate is string && data.activityDate.size() >= 2 && data.activityDate.size() <= 80 &&
             data.location is string && data.location.size() >= 2 && data.location.size() <= 200 &&
             data.photoUrls is list && data.photoUrls.size() <= 5 &&
             (data.photoUrls.size() == 0 || (data.photoUrls[0] is string && data.photoUrls[0].size() <= 220000));
    }

    function isOptionalCount(data, field) {
      return !(field in data) || data[field] == null || (data[field] is int && data[field] >= 0 && data[field] <= 100000000);
    }

    function isValidImpactMetrics(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['updatedAt']) &&
             data.keys().hasOnly([
               'peopleHelped', 'studentsSupported', 'familiesSupported',
               'madrasasSupported', 'masjidsSupported', 'updatedAt'
             ]) &&
             isOptionalCount(data, 'peopleHelped') &&
             isOptionalCount(data, 'studentsSupported') &&
             isOptionalCount(data, 'familiesSupported') &&
             isOptionalCount(data, 'madrasasSupported') &&
             isOptionalCount(data, 'masjidsSupported');
    }

    function isValidContactConfig(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll([
               'contactPerson', 'mobileNumber', 'emailAddress',
               'address', 'whatsappNumber', 'updatedAt'
             ]) &&
             data.keys().hasOnly([
               'contactPerson', 'mobileNumber', 'emailAddress',
               'address', 'whatsappNumber', 'updatedAt'
             ]) &&
             data.contactPerson is string && data.contactPerson.size() >= 1 && data.contactPerson.size() <= 150 &&
             data.mobileNumber is string && data.mobileNumber.size() >= 1 && data.mobileNumber.size() <= 40 &&
             data.emailAddress is string && data.emailAddress.size() >= 1 && data.emailAddress.size() <= 150 &&
             data.address is string && data.address.size() >= 1 && data.address.size() <= 500 &&
             data.whatsappNumber is string && data.whatsappNumber.size() <= 40;
    }

    function isValidSiteContent(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll([
               'missionStatement', 'homeIntroduction', 'aboutWhoWeAre',
               'aboutWhyCreated', 'aboutVision', 'aboutCommitment',
               'supportUsMessage', 'updatedAt'
             ]) &&
             data.keys().hasOnly([
               'missionStatement', 'homeIntroduction', 'aboutWhoWeAre',
               'aboutWhyCreated', 'aboutVision', 'aboutCommitment',
               'supportUsMessage', 'updatedAt'
             ]) &&
             data.missionStatement is string && data.missionStatement.size() >= 5 && data.missionStatement.size() <= 1000 &&
             data.homeIntroduction is string && data.homeIntroduction.size() >= 5 && data.homeIntroduction.size() <= 2000 &&
             data.aboutWhoWeAre is string && data.aboutWhoWeAre.size() >= 5 && data.aboutWhoWeAre.size() <= 3000 &&
             data.aboutWhyCreated is string && data.aboutWhyCreated.size() >= 5 && data.aboutWhyCreated.size() <= 3000 &&
             data.aboutVision is string && data.aboutVision.size() >= 5 && data.aboutVision.size() <= 3000 &&
             data.aboutCommitment is string && data.aboutCommitment.size() >= 5 && data.aboutCommitment.size() <= 3000 &&
             data.supportUsMessage is string && data.supportUsMessage.size() >= 5 && data.supportUsMessage.size() <= 1000;
    }

    function isValidLegalPolicy(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['title', 'content', 'sortOrder', 'updatedAt']) &&
             data.keys().hasOnly(['title', 'content', 'sortOrder', 'updatedAt']) &&
             data.title is string && data.title.size() >= 2 && data.title.size() <= 150 &&
             data.content is string && data.content.size() >= 10 && data.content.size() <= 15000 &&
             data.sortOrder is int && data.sortOrder >= 1 && data.sortOrder <= 50;
    }

    // --- 1. HELP REQUESTS (Strictly Private PII; Public Write-Only Intake) ---
    match /help_requests/{requestId} {
      // Ordinary visitors (unauthenticated or non-admin signed-in) can NEVER read, list, update, or delete requests.
      allow get: if isValidId(requestId) && isAdmin();
      // List uses pure token check (no get()/exists()) per cost-optimization rule.
      allow list: if isBootstrappedAdmin();

      // Visitors can submit without creating an account, locked to initial Pending Review state & empty adminNotes.
      // Verified admins may migrate existing local requests preserving review status.
      allow create: if isValidId(requestId) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasAll([
                         'referenceCode', 'fullName', 'mobileNumber', 'location',
                         'category', 'description', 'consentAccepted', 'status',
                         'adminNotes', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                         'referenceCode', 'fullName', 'mobileNumber', 'location',
                         'category', 'description', 'attachmentName', 'attachmentMimeType',
                         'attachmentDataUrl', 'consentAccepted', 'status', 'adminNotes',
                         'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().referenceCode == requestId &&
                       incoming().createdAt is timestamp && incoming().createdAt <= request.time &&
                       incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time &&
                       isValidHelpRequest(incoming()) &&
                       (
                         isAdmin() ||
                         (incoming().status == 'Pending Review' && incoming().adminNotes == '')
                       );

      // Only verified administrators can update review status and private adminNotes.
      allow update: if isValidId(requestId) &&
                       isAdmin() &&
                       isValidHelpRequest(incoming()) &&
                       incoming().referenceCode == existing().referenceCode &&
                       incoming().fullName == existing().fullName &&
                       incoming().mobileNumber == existing().mobileNumber &&
                       incoming().location == existing().location &&
                       incoming().category == existing().category &&
                       incoming().description == existing().description &&
                       incoming().consentAccepted == existing().consentAccepted &&
                       incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                       incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time &&
                       incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'adminNotes', 'updatedAt']);

      allow delete: if isValidId(requestId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // --- 2. FOUNDATION ACTIVITIES (Public Read, Admin Write) ---
    match /activities/{activityId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(activityId);
      allow list: if true;

      allow create: if isValidId(activityId) &&
                       isAdmin() &&
                       incoming().keys().hasAll([
                         'title', 'description', 'activityDate', 'location',
                         'photoUrls', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                         'title', 'description', 'activityDate', 'location',
                         'photoUrls', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().createdAt is timestamp && incoming().createdAt <= request.time &&
                       incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time &&
                       isValidActivity(incoming());

      allow update: if isValidId(activityId) &&
                       isAdmin() &&
                       isValidActivity(incoming()) &&
                       incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                       incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time &&
                       incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                         'title', 'description', 'activityDate', 'location',
                         'photoUrls', 'updatedAt'
                       ]);

      allow delete: if isValidId(activityId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // --- 3. IMPACT METRICS (Public Read, Admin Write on 'current') ---
    match /impact_metrics/{metricsId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(metricsId);
      allow list: if true;

      allow create, update: if isValidId(metricsId) &&
                               metricsId == 'current' &&
                               isAdmin() &&
                               isValidImpactMetrics(incoming()) &&
                               incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time;

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    // --- 4. CONTACT CONFIG (Public Read, Admin Write on 'current') ---
    match /contact_config/{configId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(configId);
      allow list: if true;

      allow create, update: if isValidId(configId) &&
                               configId == 'current' &&
                               isAdmin() &&
                               isValidContactConfig(incoming()) &&
                               incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time;

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    // --- 5. SITE CONTENT (Public Read, Admin Write on 'current') ---
    match /site_content/{contentId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(contentId);
      allow list: if true;

      allow create, update: if isValidId(contentId) &&
                               contentId == 'current' &&
                               isAdmin() &&
                               isValidSiteContent(incoming()) &&
                               incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time;

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    // --- 6. LEGAL POLICIES (Public Read, Admin Write) ---
    match /legal_policies/{policyKey} {
      allow get: if isValidId(policyKey);
      allow list: if true;

      allow create, update: if isValidId(policyKey) &&
                               isAdmin() &&
                               isValidLegalPolicy(incoming()) &&
                               incoming().updatedAt is timestamp && incoming().updatedAt <= request.time;

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    // --- 7. ADMIN REGISTRY (Anti-Privilege-Escalation) ---
    match /admins/{adminUid} {
      allow get: if isValidId(adminUid) && isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == adminUid;
      allow list: if false;
      allow create, update: if isValidId(adminUid) &&
                               isBootstrappedAdmin() &&
                               incoming().keys().hasAll(['email', 'grantedAt']) &&
                               incoming().keys().hasOnly(['email', 'grantedAt']) &&
                               incoming().email is string && incoming().email.size() >= 3 && incoming().email.size() <= 150 &&
                               incoming().grantedAt is timestamp && incoming().grantedAt <= request.time;
      allow delete: if isValidId(adminUid) && isBootstrappedAdmin();
    }
  }
}

const {
  initializeTestEnvironment,
  assertFails,
  assertSucceeds,
} = require("@firebase/rules-unit-testing");
const { test, before, after, beforeEach } = require("node:test");
const fs = require("node:fs");

let testEnv;
const PROJECT_ID = process.env.GCP_PROJECT || "demo-no-project";
const ADMIN_EMAIL = "faizul8057@gmail.com";
const ADMIN_UID = "admin_faizul_01";
const VISITOR_UID = "visitor_123";
const SPOOF_UID = "spoof_456";

const [emulatorHost, emulatorPortStr] = (process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST || "127.0.0.1:8085").split(":");
const emulatorPort = parseInt(emulatorPortStr, 10);

function validHelpPayload(refCode = "FF-TEST0001") {
  return {
    referenceCode: refCode,
    fullName: "Amina Begum",
    mobileNumber: "9876543210",
    location: "Rampur, Uttar Pradesh",
    category: "Education Support",
    description: "Need support for textbooks and school fees for two students.",
    consentAccepted: true,
    status: "Pending Review",
    adminNotes: "",
    createdAt: new Date(),
    updatedAt: new Date(),
  };
}

function adminContext() {
  return testEnv.authenticatedContext(ADMIN_UID, {
    email: ADMIN_EMAIL,
    email_verified: true,
  });
}

function spoofUnverifiedAdminContext() {
  return testEnv.authenticatedContext(SPOOF_UID, {
    email: ADMIN_EMAIL,
    email_verified: false,
  });
}

function ordinaryVisitorContext() {
  return testEnv.authenticatedContext(VISITOR_UID, {
    email: "ordinary.visitor@example.com",
    email_verified: true,
  });
}

before(async () => {
  const rules = fs.readFileSync("./firestore.rules", "utf8");
  testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
    projectId: PROJECT_ID,
    firestore: {
      rules,
      host: emulatorHost,
      port: emulatorPort,
    },
  });
});

after(async () => {
  if (testEnv) {
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  }
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  if (testEnv) {
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();
  }
});

// --- HELP REQUESTS: PRIVACY, RED TEAM & LIFECYCLE ---

test("Unauthenticated visitor: can submit a valid help request without login", async () => {
  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
  await assertSucceeds(
    unauthDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-TEST0001").set(validHelpPayload("FF-TEST0001"))
  );
});

test("Unauthenticated visitor: cannot read or list help requests", async () => {
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.firestore().collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV01").set(validHelpPayload("FF-PRIV01"));
  });

  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(unauthDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV01").get());
  await assertFails(unauthDb.collection("help_requests").get());
});

test("Ordinary signed-in visitor: cannot read, list, update, or delete other people's help requests", async () => {
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.firestore().collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV02").set(validHelpPayload("FF-PRIV02"));
  });

  const visitorDb = ordinaryVisitorContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(visitorDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV02").get());
  await assertFails(visitorDb.collection("help_requests").get());
  await assertFails(
    visitorDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV02").update({
      status: "Verified",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertFails(visitorDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV02").delete());
});

test("Email spoofing attack: unverified email claiming admin email is denied admin read/write", async () => {
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.firestore().collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV03").set(validHelpPayload("FF-PRIV03"));
  });

  const spoofDb = spoofUnverifiedAdminContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(spoofDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-PRIV03").get());
  await assertFails(spoofDb.collection("help_requests").get());
  await assertFails(
    spoofDb.collection("impact_metrics").doc("current").set({
      peopleHelped: 999,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
});

test("Public intake guard: rejects help request with forged status, adminNotes, ghost field, or missing consent", async () => {
  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();

  // Forged status
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-BAD01").set({
      ...validHelpPayload("FF-BAD01"),
      status: "Verified",
    })
  );

  // Forged adminNotes
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-BAD02").set({
      ...validHelpPayload("FF-BAD02"),
      adminNotes: "Pre-approved by staff",
    })
  );

  // Ghost field (Shadow Create)
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-BAD03").set({
      ...validHelpPayload("FF-BAD03"),
      isVerified: true,
    })
  );

  // Missing consent
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-BAD04").set({
      ...validHelpPayload("FF-BAD04"),
      consentAccepted: false,
    })
  );
});

test("Verified administrator: can list, read, update status & adminNotes, and delete help requests", async () => {
  await testEnv.withSecurityRulesDisabled(async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.firestore().collection("help_requests").doc("FF-ADM01").set(validHelpPayload("FF-ADM01"));
  });

  const admDb = adminContext().firestore();
  await assertSucceeds(admDb.collection("help_requests").get());
  await assertSucceeds(admDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-ADM01").get());
  await assertSucceeds(
    admDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-ADM01").update({
      status: "Under Review",
      adminNotes: "Called applicant for document verification.",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  // Shadow Update attempting to tamper with applicant fullName or inject ghost field must fail
  await assertFails(
    admDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-ADM01").update({
      fullName: "Tampered Name",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertFails(
    admDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-ADM01").update({
      status: "Verified",
      ghostField: "bad",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );

  await assertSucceeds(admDb.collection("help_requests").doc("FF-ADM01").delete());
});

// --- PUBLIC FOUNDATION COLLECTIONS (ACTIVITIES, IMPACT, CONTACT, CONTENT, POLICIES) ---

test("Public visitors can read published activities, impact, contact, content, and policies, but cannot write", async () => {
  const admDb = adminContext().firestore();
  await assertSucceeds(
    admDb.collection("activities").doc("act_001").set({
      title: "Winter Kit Distribution",
      description: "Distributed blankets and winter kits to 25 needy families.",
      activityDate: "15 Nov 2026",
      location: "Moradabad, UP",
      photoUrls: [],
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    admDb.collection("impact_metrics").doc("current").set({
      peopleHelped: 40,
      studentsSupported: 15,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    admDb.collection("contact_config").doc("current").set({
      contactPerson: "Faizul Foundation Helpline",
      mobileNumber: "7983035055",
      emailAddress: "faizulfoundation@gmail.com",
      address: "Official Office Address",
      whatsappNumber: "7983035055",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    admDb.collection("site_content").doc("current").set({
      missionStatement: "Helping poor and needy people, supporting Islamic education, and strengthening our communities.",
      homeIntroduction: "Faizul Foundation serves families and students in need.",
      aboutWhoWeAre: "Humanitarian welfare initiative.",
      aboutWhyCreated: "To bridge community needs with verified help.",
      aboutVision: "A caring, resilient community.",
      aboutCommitment: "Committed to honesty and privacy.",
      supportUsMessage: "Donations are not being accepted at this time.",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(
    admDb.collection("legal_policies").doc("privacy_policy").set({
      title: "Privacy Policy",
      content: "Faizul Foundation protects every applicant's personal information.",
      sortOrder: 1,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );

  const visitorDb = ordinaryVisitorContext().firestore();
  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();

  await assertSucceeds(unauthDb.collection("activities").get());
  await assertSucceeds(unauthDb.collection("impact_metrics").doc("current").get());
  await assertSucceeds(unauthDb.collection("contact_config").doc("current").get());
  await assertSucceeds(unauthDb.collection("site_content").doc("current").get());
  await assertSucceeds(unauthDb.collection("legal_policies").get());

  // Writes by ordinary visitors (authenticated or not) MUST fail
  await assertFails(
    visitorDb.collection("activities").doc("act_bad").set({
      title: "Fake Work",
      description: "Unauthorized write attempt.",
      activityDate: "Today",
      location: "Nowhere",
      photoUrls: [],
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb.collection("impact_metrics").doc("current").set({
      peopleHelped: 999999,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertFails(
    visitorDb.collection("contact_config").doc("current").update({
      mobileNumber: "0000000000",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
});

test("Privilege escalation guard: ordinary visitor cannot add themselves to admins collection", async () => {
  const visitorDb = ordinaryVisitorContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(
    visitorDb.collection("admins").doc(VISITOR_UID).set({
      email: "ordinary.visitor@example.com",
      grantedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
});


==========================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/auth/GoogleAuthHelper.kt
==========================================================================================
package com.example.auth

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.credentials.ClearCredentialStateRequest
import androidx.credentials.CredentialManager
import androidx.credentials.CustomCredential
import androidx.credentials.GetCredentialRequest
import androidx.credentials.exceptions.GetCredentialCancellationException
import androidx.credentials.exceptions.NoCredentialException
import com.example.R
import com.google.android.libraries.identity.googleid.GetGoogleIdOption
import com.google.android.libraries.identity.googleid.GetSignInWithGoogleOption
import com.google.android.libraries.identity.googleid.GoogleIdTokenCredential
import com.google.android.libraries.identity.googleid.GoogleIdTokenCredential.Companion.TYPE_GOOGLE_ID_TOKEN_CREDENTIAL
import com.google.firebase.Firebase
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
import com.google.firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider
import com.google.firebase.auth.auth
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

private const val AUTH_TAG = "Auth"

internal fun FirebaseAuth.authStateFlow(): Flow<FirebaseUser?> = callbackFlow {
  val listener = FirebaseAuth.AuthStateListener { firebaseAuth ->
    trySend(firebaseAuth.currentUser)
  }
  addAuthStateListener(listener)
  awaitClose { removeAuthStateListener(listener) }
}

object GoogleAuthHelper {

  fun attemptAutoSignIn(
    context: Context,
    credentialManager: CredentialManager,
    onAuthSuccess: () -> Unit,
    onUnauthenticated: () -> Unit,
    scope: CoroutineScope,
    auth: FirebaseAuth = Firebase.auth,
  ) {
    if (auth.currentUser != null) {
      onAuthSuccess()
      return
    }
    val clientId = try {
      context.getString(R.string.default_web_client_id)
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      onUnauthenticated()
      return
    }

    val googleIdOption = GetGoogleIdOption.Builder()
      .setFilterByAuthorizedAccounts(true)
      .setServerClientId(clientId)
      .setAutoSelectEnabled(true)
      .build()

    val request = GetCredentialRequest.Builder()
      .addCredentialOption(googleIdOption)
      .build()

    scope.launch {
      try {
        val result = credentialManager.getCredential(context, request)
        val credential = result.credential
        if (credential is CustomCredential && credential.type == TYPE_GOOGLE_ID_TOKEN_CREDENTIAL) {
          val googleIdToken = GoogleIdTokenCredential.createFrom(credential.data).idToken
          val authCredential = GoogleAuthProvider.getCredential(googleIdToken, null)
          auth.signInWithCredential(authCredential).await()
          onAuthSuccess()
        } else {
          onUnauthenticated()
        }
      } catch (e: NoCredentialException) {
        Log.d(AUTH_TAG, "No authorized credential cached for auto sign-in: ${e.message}")
        onUnauthenticated()
      } catch (e: Exception) {
        onUnauthenticated()
      }
    }
  }

  fun onGoogleSignInClicked(
    context: Context,
    credentialManager: CredentialManager,
    onAuthSuccess: () -> Unit,
    onAuthError: (String) -> Unit,
    scope: CoroutineScope,
    onAuthCancelled: () -> Unit = {},
    auth: FirebaseAuth = Firebase.auth,
  ) {
    val clientId = try {
      context.getString(R.string.default_web_client_id)
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      onAuthError("Google Sign-In configuration missing: default_web_client_id not found")
      return
    }

    val signInOption = GetSignInWithGoogleOption.Builder(serverClientId = clientId).build()
    val request = GetCredentialRequest.Builder()
      .addCredentialOption(signInOption)
      .build()

    scope.launch {
      try {
        val hostContext = (context as? Activity) ?: context
        val result = credentialManager.getCredential(hostContext, request)
        val credential = result.credential
        if (credential is CustomCredential && credential.type == TYPE_GOOGLE_ID_TOKEN_CREDENTIAL) {
          val googleIdToken = GoogleIdTokenCredential.createFrom(credential.data).idToken
          val authCredential = GoogleAuthProvider.getCredential(googleIdToken, null)
          auth.signInWithCredential(authCredential).await()
          onAuthSuccess()
        } else {
          onAuthError("Unexpected credential type returned by Google Sign-In.")
        }
      } catch (e: GetCredentialCancellationException) {
        Log.w(AUTH_TAG, "Google Sign-In flow cancelled or dismissed: ${e.message}", e)
        onAuthCancelled()
      } catch (e: NoCredentialException) {
        Log.w(AUTH_TAG, "No Google credential available on device: ${e.message}", e)
        onAuthError("No Google account found on this device. Please sign in to Google in Settings and try again.")
      } catch (e: Exception) {
        Log.e(AUTH_TAG, "Google Sign-In failed", e)
        onAuthError(e.localizedMessage ?: "Google Sign-In failed. Please try again.")
      }
    }
  }

  fun signOut(
    credentialManager: CredentialManager,
    scope: CoroutineScope,
    onSignOutComplete: () -> Unit = {},
    auth: FirebaseAuth = Firebase.auth,
  ) {
    scope.launch {
      withContext(NonCancellable) {
        try {
          credentialManager.clearCredentialState(ClearCredentialStateRequest())
        } catch (e: Exception) {
          Log.w(AUTH_TAG, "Failed to clear credential state: ${e.message}")
        } finally {
          auth.signOut()
          onSignOutComplete()
        }
      }
    }
  }
}


==========================================================================================
FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/remote/FirestoreErrorHandler.kt
==========================================================================================
package com.example.data.remote

import android.util.Log
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

enum class OperationType(val value: String) {
  CREATE("create"),
  UPDATE("update"),
  DELETE("delete"),
  LIST("list"),
  GET("get"),
  WRITE("write"),
}

fun handleFirestoreError(
  exception: Throwable,
  operationType: OperationType,
  path: String?,
  auth: FirebaseAuth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance(),
): String {
  val causes = generateSequence(exception) { it.cause }
  val rootError = causes.firstOrNull { it is FirebaseFirestoreException }
    ?: causes.firstOrNull { it !is CancellationException }
    ?: exception

  val currentUser = runCatching { auth.currentUser }.getOrNull()

  val providerInfoList = currentUser?.providerData?.map { provider ->
    JSONObject().apply {
      put("providerId", provider.providerId)
      put("email", provider.email)
    }
  } ?: emptyList()

  val authInfoJson = JSONObject().apply {
    put("userId", currentUser?.uid)
    put("email", currentUser?.email)
    put("emailVerified", currentUser?.isEmailVerified)
    put("tenantId", currentUser?.tenantId)
    put("providerInfo", JSONArray(providerInfoList))
  }

  val errorInfoJson = JSONObject().apply {
    put("error", rootError.message ?: rootError.toString())
    put("operationType", operationType.value)
    put("path", path)
    put("authInfo", authInfoJson)
  }

  val jsonString = errorInfoJson.toString()
  Log.e("FirestoreError", "Firestore Error: $jsonString")
  return jsonString
}

package com.example.data.remote

import com.example.data.local.ContactConfigEntity
import com.example.data.local.FoundationActivityEntity
import com.example.data.local.HelpRequestEntity
import com.example.data.local.ImpactMetricsEntity
import com.example.data.local.LegalPolicyEntity
import com.example.data.local.RequestStatus
import com.example.data.local.SiteContentEntity
import com.google.firebase.Timestamp
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentId
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.IgnoreExtraProperties
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ServerTimestamp

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class HelpRequestFirestore(
  @DocumentId val id: String = "",
  val referenceCode: String = "",
  val fullName: String = "",
  val mobileNumber: String = "",
  val location: String = "",
  val category: String = "",
  val description: String = "",
  val attachmentName: String? = null,
  val attachmentMimeType: String? = null,
  val attachmentDataUrl: String? = null,
  val consentAccepted: Boolean = true,
  val status: String = RequestStatus.PENDING_REVIEW.displayName,
  val adminNotes: String = "",
  @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
  @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null,
) {
  fun toEntity(): HelpRequestEntity {
    val resolvedRef = referenceCode.ifBlank { id }
    val createdMs = createdAt?.toDate()?.time ?: System.currentTimeMillis()
    val updatedMs = updatedAt?.toDate()?.time ?: createdMs
    return HelpRequestEntity(
      id = (resolvedRef.hashCode().toLong() and 0x7FFFFFFFL).coerceAtLeast(1L),
      referenceCode = resolvedRef,
      fullName = fullName,
      mobileNumber = mobileNumber,
      location = location,
      category = category,
      description = description,
      attachmentPath = null,
      attachmentName = attachmentName,
      attachmentMimeType = attachmentMimeType,
      attachmentDataUrl = attachmentDataUrl,
      consentAccepted = consentAccepted,
      status = status,
      adminNotes = adminNotes,
      syncedToCloud = true,
      createdAtMillis = createdMs,
      updatedAtMillis = updatedMs,
    )
  }

  companion object {
    fun toCreatePayload(
      entity: HelpRequestEntity,
      preserveAdminReviewFields: Boolean = false,
    ): Map<String, Any> {
      val raw = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
        "referenceCode" to entity.referenceCode,
        "fullName" to entity.fullName.trim(),
        "mobileNumber" to entity.mobileNumber.trim(),
        "location" to entity.location.trim(),
        "category" to entity.category,
        "description" to entity.description.trim(),
        "attachmentName" to entity.attachmentName?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.take(150),
        "attachmentMimeType" to entity.attachmentMimeType?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }?.take(100),
        "attachmentDataUrl" to entity.attachmentDataUrl?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() },
        "consentAccepted" to entity.consentAccepted,
        "status" to if (preserveAdminReviewFields) entity.status else RequestStatus.PENDING_REVIEW.displayName,
        "adminNotes" to if (preserveAdminReviewFields) entity.adminNotes.trim() else "",
        "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      )
      @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
      return raw.filterValues { it != null } as Map<String, Any>
    }
  }
}

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class ActivityFirestore(
  @DocumentId val id: String = "",
  val title: String = "",
  val description: String = "",
  val activityDate: String = "",
  val location: String = "",
  val photoUrls: List<String> = emptyList(),
  @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
  @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null,
) {
  fun toEntity(): FoundationActivityEntity {
    val createdMs = createdAt?.toDate()?.time ?: System.currentTimeMillis()
    return FoundationActivityEntity(
      id = (id.hashCode().toLong() and 0x7FFFFFFFL).coerceAtLeast(1L),
      cloudId = id,
      title = title,
      description = description,
      activityDate = activityDate,
      location = location,
      photoPathsJoined = photoUrls.joinToString("|"),
      createdAtMillis = createdMs,
    )
  }

  companion object {
    fun toCreatePayload(
      title: String,
      description: String,
      activityDate: String,
      location: String,
      photoUrls: List<String>,
    ): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
      "title" to title.trim(),
      "description" to description.trim(),
      "activityDate" to activityDate.trim(),
      "location" to location.trim(),
      "photoUrls" to photoUrls.take(5),
      "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    )

    fun toUpdatePayload(
      title: String,
      description: String,
      activityDate: String,
      location: String,
      photoUrls: List<String>,
    ): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
      "title" to title.trim(),
      "description" to description.trim(),
      "activityDate" to activityDate.trim(),
      "location" to location.trim(),
      "photoUrls" to photoUrls.take(5),
      "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    )
  }
}

object FirestoreDocMappers {
  fun DocumentSnapshot.toImpactMetricsOrNull(): ImpactMetricsEntity? {
    if (!exists()) return null
    val updated = getTimestamp("updatedAt", DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE)
      ?.toDate()?.time
    return ImpactMetricsEntity(
      id = 1,
      peopleHelped = getLong("peopleHelped")?.toInt(),
      studentsSupported = getLong("studentsSupported")?.toInt(),
      familiesSupported = getLong("familiesSupported")?.toInt(),
      madrasasSupported = getLong("madrasasSupported")?.toInt(),
      masjidsSupported = getLong("masjidsSupported")?.toInt(),
      lastVerifiedAtMillis = updated,
    )
  }

  fun ImpactMetricsEntity.toFirestoreMap(): Map<String, Any> {
    val raw = mutableMapOf<String, Any?>(
      "peopleHelped" to peopleHelped,
      "studentsSupported" to studentsSupported,
      "familiesSupported" to familiesSupported,
      "madrasasSupported" to madrasasSupported,
      "masjidsSupported" to masjidsSupported,
      "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    )
    @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
    return raw.filterValues { it != null } as Map<String, Any>
  }

  fun DocumentSnapshot.toContactConfigOrNull(): ContactConfigEntity? {
    if (!exists()) return null
    return ContactConfigEntity(
      id = 1,
      contactPerson = getString("contactPerson") ?: return null,
      mobileNumber = getString("mobileNumber") ?: "",
      emailAddress = getString("emailAddress") ?: "",
      address = getString("address") ?: "",
      whatsappNumber = getString("whatsappNumber") ?: "",
    )
  }

  fun ContactConfigEntity.toFirestoreMap(): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
    "contactPerson" to contactPerson.trim(),
    "mobileNumber" to mobileNumber.trim(),
    "emailAddress" to emailAddress.trim(),
    "address" to address.trim(),
    "whatsappNumber" to whatsappNumber.trim(),
    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  )

  fun DocumentSnapshot.toSiteContentOrNull(): SiteContentEntity? {
    if (!exists()) return null
    return SiteContentEntity(
      id = 1,
      missionStatement = getString("missionStatement") ?: return null,
      homeIntroduction = getString("homeIntroduction") ?: "",
      aboutWhoWeAre = getString("aboutWhoWeAre") ?: "",
      aboutWhyCreated = getString("aboutWhyCreated") ?: "",
      aboutVision = getString("aboutVision") ?: "",
      aboutCommitment = getString("aboutCommitment") ?: "",
      supportUsMessage = getString("supportUsMessage") ?: "",
    )
  }

  fun SiteContentEntity.toFirestoreMap(): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
    "missionStatement" to missionStatement.trim(),
    "homeIntroduction" to homeIntroduction.trim(),
    "aboutWhoWeAre" to aboutWhoWeAre.trim(),
    "aboutWhyCreated" to aboutWhyCreated.trim(),
    "aboutVision" to aboutVision.trim(),
    "aboutCommitment" to aboutCommitment.trim(),
    "supportUsMessage" to supportUsMessage.trim(),
    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  )

  fun DocumentSnapshot.toLegalPolicyOrNull(): LegalPolicyEntity? {
    if (!exists()) return null
    val title = getString("title") ?: return null
    val content = getString("content") ?: return null
    val sortOrder = (getLong("sortOrder") ?: 1L).toInt()
    val updatedMs = getTimestamp("updatedAt", DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE)
      ?.toDate()?.time ?: System.currentTimeMillis()
    return LegalPolicyEntity(
      policyKey = id,
      title = title,
      content = content,
      sortOrder = sortOrder,
      lastUpdatedMillis = updatedMs,
    )
  }

  fun LegalPolicyEntity.toFirestoreMap(): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
    "title" to title.trim(),
    "content" to content.trim(),
    "sortOrder" to sortOrder,
    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  )
}

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/remote/FirebaseFoundationDataSource.kt

package com.example.data.remote

import android.content.Context
import com.example.R
import com.example.data.local.ContactConfigEntity
import com.example.data.local.FoundationActivityEntity
import com.example.data.local.HelpRequestEntity
import com.example.data.local.ImpactMetricsEntity
import com.example.data.local.LegalPolicyEntity
import com.example.data.local.RequestStatus
import com.example.data.local.SiteContentEntity
import com.example.data.remote.FirestoreDocMappers.toContactConfigOrNull
import com.example.data.remote.FirestoreDocMappers.toFirestoreMap
import com.example.data.remote.FirestoreDocMappers.toImpactMetricsOrNull
import com.example.data.remote.FirestoreDocMappers.toLegalPolicyOrNull
import com.example.data.remote.FirestoreDocMappers.toSiteContentOrNull
import com.google.firebase.Firebase
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseUser
import com.google.firebase.auth.auth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.snapshots
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.emitAll
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await

class FirebaseFoundationDataSource(
  private val db: FirebaseFirestore,
  private val auth: FirebaseAuth = Firebase.auth,
) {
  constructor(context: Context) : this(
    db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(
      context.applicationContext.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
    ),
    auth = Firebase.auth,
  )

  fun currentUser(): FirebaseUser? = auth.currentUser

  fun isBootstrappedAdmin(user: FirebaseUser? = auth.currentUser): Boolean {
    return user != null &&
      user.email.equals(ADMIN_EMAIL, ignoreCase = true) &&
      user.isEmailVerified
  }

  suspend fun checkIsAuthorizedAdmin(): Boolean {
    val user = auth.currentUser ?: return false
    if (isBootstrappedAdmin(user)) return true
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_ADMINS).document(user.uid)
    return try {
      docRef.get().await().exists()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path, auth)
      false
    }
  }

  // --- PUBLIC REAL-TIME OBSERVERS ---

  fun observeActivities(): Flow<List<FoundationActivityEntity>> =
    db.collection(COLLECTION_ACTIVITIES)
      .snapshots()
      .map { snapshot ->
        snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { doc ->
          doc.toObject(
            ActivityFirestore::class.java,
            DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE,
          )?.toEntity()
        }.sortedByDescending { it.createdAtMillis }
      }
      .catch { error ->
        logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error, OperationType.LIST, COLLECTION_ACTIVITIES)
        throw error
      }

  fun observeImpactMetrics(): Flow<ImpactMetricsEntity?> =
    db.collection(COLLECTION_IMPACT).document(DOC_CURRENT)
      .snapshots()
      .map { snapshot -> snapshot.toImpactMetricsOrNull() }
      .catch { error ->
        logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error, OperationType.GET, "$COLLECTION_IMPACT/$DOC_CURRENT")
        throw error
      }

  fun observeContactConfig(): Flow<ContactConfigEntity?> =
    db.collection(COLLECTION_CONTACT).document(DOC_CURRENT)
      .snapshots()
      .map { snapshot -> snapshot.toContactConfigOrNull() }
      .catch { error ->
        logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error, OperationType.GET, "$COLLECTION_CONTACT/$DOC_CURRENT")
        throw error
      }

  fun observeSiteContent(): Flow<SiteContentEntity?> =
    db.collection(COLLECTION_SITE_CONTENT).document(DOC_CURRENT)
      .snapshots()
      .map { snapshot -> snapshot.toSiteContentOrNull() }
      .catch { error ->
        logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error, OperationType.GET, "$COLLECTION_SITE_CONTENT/$DOC_CURRENT")
        throw error
      }

  fun observeLegalPolicies(): Flow<List<LegalPolicyEntity>> =
    db.collection(COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES)
      .snapshots()
      .map { snapshot ->
        snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { it.toLegalPolicyOrNull() }
          .sortedBy { it.sortOrder }
      }
      .catch { error ->
        logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error, OperationType.LIST, COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES)
        throw error
      }

  fun observeLegalPolicy(policyKey: String): Flow<LegalPolicyEntity?> =
    db.collection(COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES).document(policyKey)
      .snapshots()
      .map { snapshot -> snapshot.toLegalPolicyOrNull() }
      .catch { error ->
        logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error, OperationType.GET, "$COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES/$policyKey")
        throw error
      }

  // --- ADMIN-ONLY HELP REQUESTS OBSERVER (Strictly gated behind Admin Auth) ---

  fun observeHelpRequestsForAdmin(): Flow<List<HelpRequestEntity>> = flow {
    val activeUid = auth.currentUser?.uid
      ?: throw IllegalStateException("Administrator must be signed in before reading help requests.")
    emitAll(
      db.collection(COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS)
        .snapshots()
        .map { snapshot ->
          snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { doc ->
            doc.toObject(
              HelpRequestFirestore::class.java,
              DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE,
            )?.toEntity()
          }.sortedByDescending { it.createdAtMillis }
        }
        .catch { error ->
          val firestoreError = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }
            .filterIsInstance<FirebaseFirestoreException>()
            .firstOrNull()
          if (firestoreError == null && error is CancellationException) throw error
          if (auth.currentUser?.uid == activeUid) {
            handleFirestoreError(firestoreError ?: error, OperationType.LIST, COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS, auth)
          }
          throw error
        }
    )
  }

  // --- PUBLIC HELP REQUEST SUBMISSION (Write-Only Intake) ---

  suspend fun submitHelpRequest(
    entity: HelpRequestEntity,
    preserveAdminReviewFields: Boolean = false,
  ) {
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS).document(entity.referenceCode)
    val payload = HelpRequestFirestore.toCreatePayload(entity, preserveAdminReviewFields)
    try {
      docRef.set(payload).await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.CREATE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun getHelpRequestForAdmin(referenceCode: String): HelpRequestEntity? {
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS).document(referenceCode)
    return try {
      val snap = docRef.get().await()
      snap.toObject(
        HelpRequestFirestore::class.java,
        DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE,
      )?.toEntity()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  // --- ADMIN HELP REQUEST REVIEW & DELETE ---

  suspend fun updateHelpRequestReview(
    referenceCode: String,
    newStatus: RequestStatus,
    adminNotes: String,
  ) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS).document(referenceCode)
    val payload = mapOf(
      "status" to newStatus.displayName,
      "adminNotes" to adminNotes.trim(),
      "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    )
    try {
      docRef.update(payload).await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.UPDATE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun deleteHelpRequest(referenceCode: String) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS).document(referenceCode)
    try {
      docRef.delete().await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.DELETE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  // --- ADMIN ACTIVITIES, IMPACT, CONTACT, CONTENT, POLICIES ---

  suspend fun saveActivity(
    activityId: String,
    title: String,
    description: String,
    activityDate: String,
    location: String,
    photoUrls: List<String>,
    isUpdate: Boolean,
  ) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_ACTIVITIES).document(activityId)
    try {
      if (isUpdate && docRef.get().await().exists()) {
        docRef.update(
          ActivityFirestore.toUpdatePayload(title, description, activityDate, location, photoUrls)
        ).await()
      } else {
        docRef.set(
          ActivityFirestore.toCreatePayload(title, description, activityDate, location, photoUrls)
        ).await()
      }
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, if (isUpdate) OperationType.UPDATE else OperationType.CREATE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun deleteActivity(activityId: String) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_ACTIVITIES).document(activityId)
    try {
      docRef.delete().await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.DELETE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun upsertImpactMetrics(metrics: ImpactMetricsEntity) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_IMPACT).document(DOC_CURRENT)
    try {
      docRef.set(metrics.toFirestoreMap()).await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun upsertContactConfig(config: ContactConfigEntity) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_CONTACT).document(DOC_CURRENT)
    try {
      docRef.set(config.toFirestoreMap()).await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun upsertSiteContent(content: SiteContentEntity) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_SITE_CONTENT).document(DOC_CURRENT)
    try {
      docRef.set(content.toFirestoreMap()).await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  suspend fun upsertLegalPolicy(policy: LegalPolicyEntity) {
    requireUserSignedIn()
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES).document(policy.policyKey)
    try {
      docRef.set(policy.toFirestoreMap()).await()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, docRef.path, auth)
      throw e
    }
  }

  // --- HELPER EXISTENCE CHECKS FOR SAFE NON-DESTRUCTIVE MIGRATION ---

  suspend fun helpRequestExists(referenceCode: String): Boolean {
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS).document(referenceCode)
    return try {
      docRef.get().await().exists()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path, auth)
      false
    }
  }

  suspend fun activityExists(activityId: String): Boolean {
    val docRef = db.collection(COLLECTION_ACTIVITIES).document(activityId)
    return try {
      docRef.get().await().exists()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path, auth)
      false
    }
  }

  suspend fun impactExists(): Boolean = documentExists(COLLECTION_IMPACT, DOC_CURRENT)
  suspend fun contactExists(): Boolean = documentExists(COLLECTION_CONTACT, DOC_CURRENT)
  suspend fun siteContentExists(): Boolean = documentExists(COLLECTION_SITE_CONTENT, DOC_CURRENT)
  suspend fun legalPolicyExists(policyKey: String): Boolean =
    documentExists(COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES, policyKey)

  private suspend fun documentExists(collection: String, docId: String): Boolean {
    val docRef = db.collection(collection).document(docId)
    return try {
      docRef.get().await().exists()
    } catch (e: CancellationException) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Exception) {
      handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path, auth)
      false
    }
  }

  private fun requireUserSignedIn(): String {
    return auth.currentUser?.uid
      ?: throw IllegalStateException("Administrator must be signed in with Google.")
  }

  private fun logSnapshotErrorIfNeeded(error: Throwable, op: OperationType, path: String) {
    val firestoreError = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }
      .filterIsInstance<FirebaseFirestoreException>()
      .firstOrNull()
    if (firestoreError == null && error is CancellationException) return
    handleFirestoreError(firestoreError ?: error, op, path, auth)
  }

  companion object {
    const val ADMIN_EMAIL = "faizul8057@gmail.com"
    const val COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS = "help_requests"
    const val COLLECTION_ACTIVITIES = "activities"
    const val COLLECTION_IMPACT = "impact_metrics"
    const val COLLECTION_CONTACT = "contact_config"
    const val COLLECTION_SITE_CONTENT = "site_content"
    const val COLLECTION_LEGAL_POLICIES = "legal_policies"
    const val COLLECTION_ADMINS = "admins"
    const val DOC_CURRENT = "current"
  }
}

FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/base/FirestoreEmulatorTestBase.kt

package com.example.base

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.R
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseApp
import com.google.firebase.FirebaseOptions
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreSettings
import com.google.firebase.firestore.MemoryCacheSettings
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import org.robolectric.annotation.LooperMode
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(manifest = Config.NONE)
@LooperMode(LooperMode.Mode.INSTRUMENTATION_TEST)
abstract class FirestoreEmulatorTestBase {

  protected lateinit var context: Context
  protected lateinit var firestore: FirebaseFirestore
  protected lateinit var auth: FirebaseAuth
  protected lateinit var databaseId: String

  @Before
  open fun setUpFirebase() {
    context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
    val resId = context.resources.getIdentifier("firestore_database_id", "string", context.packageName)
    databaseId = when {
      resId != 0 -> context.getString(resId)
      else -> runCatching { context.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id) }
        .getOrElse { "ai-studio-android-faizulfo-f0f6a0d8-3b67-49ac-b440-68d0176935a6" }
    }

    val app = if (FirebaseApp.getApps(context).isEmpty()) {
      val newApp = FirebaseApp.initializeApp(
        context,
        FirebaseOptions.Builder()
          .setApplicationId("com.aistudio.faizulfoundation.krmxvp")
          .setProjectId(PROJECT_ID)
          .setApiKey(FAKE_API_KEY)
          .build(),
      )
      val newFirestore = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(newApp, databaseId)
      newFirestore.useEmulator(EMULATOR_HOST, FIRESTORE_PORT)
      newFirestore.firestoreSettings = FirebaseFirestoreSettings.Builder()
        .setLocalCacheSettings(MemoryCacheSettings.newBuilder().build())
        .build()

      val newAuth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance(newApp)
      newAuth.useEmulator(EMULATOR_HOST, AUTH_PORT)
      newApp
    } else {
      FirebaseApp.getInstance()
    }

    firestore = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(app, databaseId)
    auth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance(app)
  }

  @After
  open fun tearDownFirebase() {
    auth.signOut()
  }

  protected suspend fun signInTestUser(email: String): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
    withTimeout(AUTH_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      val result = try {
        auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).await()
      } catch (unused: FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException) {
        auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).await()
      }
      checkNotNull(result.user?.uid) { "User auth failed for $email" }
    }
  }

  protected suspend fun signInVerifiedAdmin(
    email: String = ADMIN_EMAIL,
  ): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
    withTimeout(AUTH_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      val result = try {
        auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).await()
      } catch (unused: FirebaseAuthInvalidUserException) {
        auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email, DEFAULT_PASSWORD).await()
      }
      val user = checkNotNull(result.user) { "Admin auth returned null user" }
      if (!user.isEmailVerified) {
        verifyUserEmailInEmulator(user.email ?: email, user.getIdToken(false).await().token!!)
        user.reload().await()
        user.getIdToken(true).await()
      }
      check(user.isEmailVerified) { "Failed to mark emulator user emailVerified=true for $email" }
      user.uid
    }
  }

  private fun verifyUserEmailInEmulator(email: String, idToken: String) {
    val sendOobUrl =
      "http://$EMULATOR_HOST:$AUTH_PORT/identitytoolkit.googleapis.com/v1/accounts:sendOobCode?key=$FAKE_API_KEY"
    postJson(
      sendOobUrl,
      JSONObject()
        .put("requestType", "VERIFY_EMAIL")
        .put("idToken", idToken)
        .toString(),
    )

    val oobCodesUrl =
      "http://$EMULATOR_HOST:$AUTH_PORT/emulator/v1/projects/$PROJECT_ID/oobCodes"
    val oobJson = JSONObject(getText(oobCodesUrl))
    val oobArray: JSONArray = oobJson.optJSONArray("oobCodes") ?: JSONArray()
    var matchedCode: String? = null
    for (i in oobArray.length() - 1 downTo 0) {
      val entry = oobArray.getJSONObject(i)
      if (entry.optString("email").equals(email, ignoreCase = true) &&
        entry.optString("requestType") == "VERIFY_EMAIL"
      ) {
        matchedCode = entry.optString("oobCode")
        break
      }
    }
    check(!matchedCode.isNullOrBlank()) { "No VERIFY_EMAIL oobCode generated in Auth emulator for $email" }

    val applyOobUrl =
      "http://$EMULATOR_HOST:$AUTH_PORT/identitytoolkit.googleapis.com/v1/accounts:update?key=$FAKE_API_KEY"
    postJson(applyOobUrl, JSONObject().put("oobCode", matchedCode).toString())
  }

  private fun postJson(urlStr: String, body: String): String {
    val conn = (URL(urlStr).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
      requestMethod = "POST"
      doOutput = true
      setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json")
      connectTimeout = 3000
      readTimeout = 3000
    }
    conn.outputStream.use { it.write(body.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)) }
    val code = conn.responseCode
    val stream = if (code in 200..299) conn.inputStream else conn.errorStream
    val resp = stream?.bufferedReader()?.readText().orEmpty()
    check(code in 200..299) { "HTTP $code from $urlStr: $resp" }
    return resp
  }

  private fun getText(urlStr: String): String {
    val conn = (URL(urlStr).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
      requestMethod = "GET"
      connectTimeout = 3000
      readTimeout = 3000
    }
    val code = conn.responseCode
    val stream = if (code in 200..299) conn.inputStream else conn.errorStream
    val resp = stream?.bufferedReader()?.readText().orEmpty()
    check(code in 200..299) { "HTTP $code from $urlStr: $resp" }
    return resp
  }

  protected fun Throwable.unwrapFirestoreException(): FirebaseFirestoreException? {
    var current: Throwable? = this
    while (current != null) {
      if (current is FirebaseFirestoreException) return current
      current = current.cause
    }
    return null
  }

  protected suspend fun assertPermissionDenied(block: suspend () -> Unit) {
    try {
      block()
      throw AssertionError("Expected FirebaseFirestoreException(PERMISSION_DENIED), but operation succeeded.")
    } catch (e: AssertionError) {
      throw e
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
      val firestoreException = checkNotNull(e.unwrapFirestoreException()) {
        "Expected nested FirebaseFirestoreException(PERMISSION_DENIED), got $e"
      }
      if (firestoreException.code != FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.PERMISSION_DENIED) {
        throw AssertionError(
          "Expected PERMISSION_DENIED, got ${firestoreException.code}: ${firestoreException.message}",
          e,
        )
      }
    }
  }

  companion object {
    const val EMULATOR_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
    const val FIRESTORE_PORT = 8085
    const val AUTH_PORT = 9099
    const val PROJECT_ID = "demo-no-project"
    const val FAKE_API_KEY = "[REDACTED]"
    const val DEFAULT_PASSWORD = "[REDACTED]"
    const val AUTH_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000L
    const val ADMIN_EMAIL = "faizul8057@gmail.com"
  }
}

package com.example.data.repository

import androidx.room.Room
import com.example.base.FirestoreEmulatorTestBase
import com.example.data.local.AdminSecurityManager
import com.example.data.local.AttachmentStorage
import com.example.data.local.ContactConfigEntity
import com.example.data.local.FaizulDatabase
import com.example.data.local.FoundationActivityEntity
import com.example.data.local.FoundationRepository
import com.example.data.local.HelpCategory
import com.example.data.local.HelpRequestEntity
import com.example.data.local.ImpactMetricsEntity
import com.example.data.local.LegalPolicyEntity
import com.example.data.local.RequestStatus
import com.example.data.local.SiteContentEntity
import com.example.data.remote.FirebaseFoundationDataSource
import com.google.firebase.firestore.snapshots
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import java.util.UUID

class FoundationFirestoreRuleTest : FirestoreEmulatorTestBase() {

  private lateinit var database: FaizulDatabase
  private lateinit var dataSource: FirebaseFoundationDataSource
  private lateinit var repository: FoundationRepository

  @Before
  fun setUpRepo() {
    database = Room.inMemoryDatabaseBuilder(context, FaizulDatabase::class.java)
      .allowMainThreadQueries()
      .build()
    dataSource = FirebaseFoundationDataSource(firestore, auth)
    repository = FoundationRepository(
      database = database,
      securityManager = AdminSecurityManager(context),
      attachmentStorage = AttachmentStorage(context),
      remoteDataSource = dataSource,
    )
  }

  @After
  fun tearDownRepo() {
    database.close()
  }

  @Test
  fun submitHelpRequest_unauthenticatedVisitor_succeedsPrivately() = runBlocking {
    auth.signOut()

    val result = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      repository.submitHelpRequest(
        fullName = "Zaid Khan",
        mobileNumber = "9876543210",
        location = "Moradabad, UP",
        category = HelpCategory.EDUCATION_SUPPORT,
        description = "Requesting school books and fees support for two orphans in our neighborhood.",
        attachmentUri = null,
        consentAccepted = true,
      )
    }
    assertTrue("Public help request submission should succeed: ${result.exceptionOrNull()}", result.isSuccess)
    val refCode = result.getOrThrow()
    assertTrue(refCode.startsWith("FF-"))

    // Ordinary visitor cannot read back what they or anyone else submitted
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.getHelpRequestForAdmin(refCode)
      }
    }

    // Verified admin can inspect it
    signInVerifiedAdmin()
    val stored = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.getHelpRequestForAdmin(refCode) }
    assertNotNull(stored)
    assertEquals("Zaid Khan", stored!!.fullName)
    assertEquals(RequestStatus.PENDING_REVIEW.displayName, stored.status)
    assertEquals("", stored.adminNotes)

    // Clean up test document
    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.deleteHelpRequest(refCode) }
  }

  @Test
  fun submitHelpRequest_unauthenticatedVisitor_cannotSpoofAdminStatusOrNotes() = runBlocking {
    auth.signOut()
    val refCode = "FF-SPOOF-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(8).uppercase()}"
    val spoofed = HelpRequestEntity(
      referenceCode = refCode,
      fullName = "Spoof Caller",
      mobileNumber = "9876543210",
      location = "Delhi",
      category = HelpCategory.FINANCIAL_ASSISTANCE.displayName,
      description = "Attempting to bypass initial Pending Review state on create.",
      consentAccepted = true,
      status = RequestStatus.ASSISTED.displayName,
      adminNotes = "Self-approved",
    )

    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.submitHelpRequest(spoofed, preserveAdminReviewFields = true)
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  fun helpRequests_unauthenticatedListSnapshot_failsWithPermissionDenied() = runBlocking {
    auth.signOut()

    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        firestore.collection(FirebaseFoundationDataSource.COLLECTION_HELP_REQUESTS)
          .snapshots()
          .first()
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  fun helpRequests_signedInOrdinaryVisitor_cannotReadListUpdateOrDelete() = runBlocking {
    val refCode = "FF-PRIV-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(8).uppercase()}"
    signInVerifiedAdmin()
    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.submitHelpRequest(
        HelpRequestEntity(
          referenceCode = refCode,
          fullName = "Private Applicant",
          mobileNumber = "9123456789",
          location = "Bareilly, UP",
          category = HelpCategory.FOOD_AND_BASIC_NEEDS.displayName,
          description = "Urgent ration assistance needed for elderly widow family.",
          consentAccepted = true,
        )
      )
    }

    // Switch to ordinary signed-in visitor (non-admin)
    signInTestUser(VISITOR_EMAIL)

    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.observeHelpRequestsForAdmin().first()
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.getHelpRequestForAdmin(refCode)
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.updateHelpRequestReview(refCode, RequestStatus.VERIFIED, "Unauthorized note")
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.deleteHelpRequest(refCode)
      }
    }

    signInVerifiedAdmin()
    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.deleteHelpRequest(refCode) }
  }

  @Test
  fun helpRequests_unverifiedAdminEmail_failsWithPermissionDenied() = runBlocking {
    // Sign in with unverified faizul8057@gmail.com account (or separate unverified email if already verified)
    signInTestUser("unverified.spoof@test.com")

    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.observeHelpRequestsForAdmin().first()
      }
    }
  }

  @Test
  fun adminReview_verifiedAdmin_observesUpdatesAndDeletesRequest() = runBlocking {
    auth.signOut()
    val refCode = "FF-REV-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(8).uppercase()}"
    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.submitHelpRequest(
        HelpRequestEntity(
          referenceCode = refCode,
          fullName = "Bilal Ahmed",
          mobileNumber = "7983035055",
          location = "Rampur, UP",
          category = HelpCategory.MADRASA_SUPPORT.displayName,
          description = "Roof repair and classroom mats needed for local village madrasa.",
          consentAccepted = true,
        )
      )
    }

    signInVerifiedAdmin()

    val requests = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      repository.observeHelpRequestsForAdmin().first { list -> list.any { it.referenceCode == refCode } }
    }
    val created = requests.first { it.referenceCode == refCode }
    assertEquals(RequestStatus.PENDING_REVIEW.displayName, created.status)

    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      repository.updateHelpRequestReview(
        request = created,
        newStatus = RequestStatus.UNDER_REVIEW,
        adminNotes = "Field team contacted local imam.",
      )
    }

    val updated = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.getHelpRequestForAdmin(refCode) }
    assertNotNull(updated)
    assertEquals(RequestStatus.UNDER_REVIEW.displayName, updated!!.status)
    assertEquals("Field team contacted local imam.", updated.adminNotes)

    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { repository.deleteHelpRequest(updated) }
    val afterDelete = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.getHelpRequestForAdmin(refCode) }
    assertNull(afterDelete)
  }

  @Test
  fun publicContent_visitorDeniedWrite_verifiedAdminWritesAndPublicReads() = runBlocking {
    val actId = "act_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").take(12)}"

    // Ordinary visitor cannot mutate any public foundation collections
    signInTestUser(VISITOR_EMAIL)
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.saveActivity(
          activityId = actId,
          title = "Unauthorized Activity",
          description = "Should be blocked by Firestore security rules.",
          activityDate = "10 Nov 2026",
          location = "Test",
          photoUrls = emptyList(),
          isUpdate = false,
        )
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.upsertImpactMetrics(ImpactMetricsEntity(peopleHelped = 999))
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.upsertContactConfig(FoundationRepository.defaultContact())
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.upsertSiteContent(FoundationRepository.defaultSiteContent())
      }
    }
    assertPermissionDenied {
      withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        dataSource.upsertLegalPolicy(
          LegalPolicyEntity(
            policyKey = "privacy_policy",
            title = "Privacy Policy",
            content = "Tampered policy text.",
            sortOrder = 1,
          )
        )
      }
    }

    // Verified admin publishes verified activity, impact, contact & content
    signInVerifiedAdmin()
    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.saveActivity(
        activityId = actId,
        title = "Winter Blanket Distribution",
        description = "Distributed 40 woollen blankets to elderly families after Isha.",
        activityDate = "15 Nov 2026",
        location = "Sambhal, UP",
        photoUrls = emptyList(),
        isUpdate = false,
      )
      dataSource.upsertImpactMetrics(
        ImpactMetricsEntity(
          peopleHelped = 40,
          studentsSupported = 12,
          familiesSupported = 18,
          madrasasSupported = 2,
          masjidsSupported = 1,
        )
      )
      dataSource.upsertContactConfig(
        ContactConfigEntity(
          contactPerson = "Faizul Foundation Helpline",
          mobileNumber = "7983035055",
          emailAddress = "faizulfoundation@gmail.com",
          address = "Sambhal, Uttar Pradesh",
          whatsappNumber = "7983035055",
        )
      )
      dataSource.upsertSiteContent(FoundationRepository.defaultSiteContent())
    }

    // Unauthenticated public visitor can read published collections in real time
    auth.signOut()
    val publicActivities = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.observeActivities().first { list -> list.any { it.cloudId == actId } }
    }
    assertTrue(publicActivities.any { it.title == "Winter Blanket Distribution" })

    val publicImpact = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.observeImpactMetrics().first { it?.peopleHelped == 40 }
    }
    assertEquals(18, publicImpact?.familiesSupported)

    val publicContact = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.observeContactConfig().first { it?.emailAddress == "faizulfoundation@gmail.com" }
    }
    assertEquals("7983035055", publicContact?.mobileNumber)

    signInVerifiedAdmin()
    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.deleteActivity(actId) }
  }

  @Test
  fun migrateLocalDataToCloudIfNeeded_migratesOnceWithoutDuplicates() = runBlocking {
    val localReqRef = "FF-MIG-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(8).uppercase()}"
    database.helpRequestDao().insert(
      HelpRequestEntity(
        referenceCode = localReqRef,
        fullName = "Local Seeded Applicant",
        mobileNumber = "9811112222",
        location = "Aligarh, UP",
        category = HelpCategory.MASJID_SUPPORT.displayName,
        description = "Pre-existing local room request requiring solar inverter batteries.",
        consentAccepted = true,
        status = RequestStatus.VERIFIED.displayName,
        adminNotes = "Already reviewed locally prior to cloud migration.",
        syncedToCloud = false,
      )
    )
    database.activityDao().insert(
      FoundationActivityEntity(
        cloudId = "",
        title = "Pre-existing Local School Kit Drive",
        description = "Local record created prior to Firebase integration.",
        activityDate = "01 Nov 2026",
        location = "Aligarh, UP",
        photoPathsJoined = "",
      )
    )

    signInVerifiedAdmin()
    assertFalse(dataSource.helpRequestExists(localReqRef))

    val firstCount = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      repository.migrateLocalDataToCloudIfNeeded()
    }
    assertTrue("Expected >= 2 migrated items, got $firstCount", firstCount >= 2)
    assertTrue(dataSource.helpRequestExists(localReqRef))

    val migratedReq = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) { dataSource.getHelpRequestForAdmin(localReqRef) }
    assertNotNull(migratedReq)
    assertEquals(RequestStatus.VERIFIED.displayName, migratedReq!!.status)
    assertEquals("Already reviewed locally prior to cloud migration.", migratedReq.adminNotes)

    // Local Room row should now be flagged syncedToCloud = true
    val updatedLocalReq = database.helpRequestDao().getByReferenceCode(localReqRef)
    assertTrue(updatedLocalReq?.syncedToCloud == true)

    val localAct = database.activityDao().getAllOnce().first()
    assertTrue(localAct.cloudId.startsWith("act_"))
    assertTrue(dataSource.activityExists(localAct.cloudId))

    // Second migration pass must be a no-op (0 items re-uploaded)
    val secondCount = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      repository.migrateLocalDataToCloudIfNeeded()
    }
    assertEquals(0, secondCount)

    withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      dataSource.deleteHelpRequest(localReqRef)
      dataSource.deleteActivity(localAct.cloudId)
    }
  }

  private companion object {
    const val VISITOR_EMAIL = "visitor@test.com"
    const val DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000L
    const val FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000L
  }
}

# Run and deploy your AI Studio app

This contains everything you need to run your app locally.

View your app in AI Studio: https://ai.studio/apps/f0f6a0d8-3b67-49ac-b440-68d0176935a6

## Run Locally

**Prerequisites:**  [Android Studio](https://developer.android.com/studio)


1. Open Android Studio
2. Select **Open** and choose the directory containing this project
3. Allow Android Studio to fix any incompatibilities as it imports the project.
4. Create a file named `.env` in the project directory and set `GEMINI_API_KEY` in that file to your Gemini API key (see `.env.example` for an example)
5. Remove this line from the app's `build.gradle.kts` file: `signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("debugConfig")`
6. Run the app on an emulator or physical device
7. If you have already published your app in AI Studio, please [request upload key reset](https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/9842756#zippy=%2Crequest-an-upload-key-reset) in Google Play Console.
